CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW fng_v_actl_attnd_prcnt
SELECT ALL brn_year,
emp_no,
( SUM (absnt_totl)
/ DECODE (SUM (stndr_day_hours),
0, 1,
SUM (stndr_day_hours)
* 100 absent,
( SUM (a_day_hours)
* 100 attend,
0 late,
0 extra_assignment,
0 holiday,
0 permission,
0 tasks,
ad_u_id,
ad_date,
ad_trmnl_nm
FROM fng_actl_clc_mst
GROUP BY brn_year,

CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW fng_v_clc_artcl_dtl3
SELECT m.brn_no,
p.emp_l_nm,
p.emp_f_nm,
(CASE
WHEN m.day_off_flg = 1
THEN 'DO'
WHEN m.official_hldy_flg = 1
THEN 'OH'
WHEN m.hldy_flg = 1
THEN 'HOL'
WHEN NVL (m.absnt_flg, 0) = 0
THEN 'P/PR'
WHEN m.tsk_flg = 1
THEN 'P/TSK'
ELSE 'P'
ELSE 'AB'
) clc_symbol,
THEN 'DO-DAY OFF'
THEN 'OH-OFFICIAL HOLIDAY'
THEN 'HOL
-HOLIDAY' || '- HOLDAY TYPE =' || m.hldy_no
THEN 'P-PRESENT/PR-PERMISSION'
THEN 'P-PRESENT/TSK-TASK WORK'
ELSE 'P-PRESENT'
THEN 'ASGN-EXTRA ASSIGNMENT/PR-PERMISSION'
ELSE 'ASGN-EXTRA ASSIGNMENT'
ELSE 'AB-ABSENT'
) clc_desc,
m.a_time_in,
m.a_time_out,
m.a_day_hours,
m.da
y_off_flg,
D.RTRV_FLG
FROM --hrs_artcl a,
fng_actl_clc_mst m LEFT OUTER JOIN fng_actl_clc_dtl d
ON (m.stndr_srl = d.stndr_srl AND m.emp_no = d.emp_no)
WHERE m.emp_no = p.emp_no AND m.stndr_day_hours IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY m.a_day_hours,
period_factor,
stndr_day_hours,
m.absnt_flg,
m.hldy_flg,
m.prmsn_flg,
m.tsk_flg,
----------------------------------------------------------------
THEN 'HOL-HOLIDAY' || '- HOLDAY TYPE =' || m.hldy_no
.Fng_actl_clc_mst m LEFT OUTER JOIN
.Fng_actl_clc_dtl d

CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER FNG_ACTL_CLC_DTL_INSRT
BEFORE INSERT
ON FNG_ACTL_CLC_DTL
REFERENCING NEW AS New OLD AS OLD
FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE
V_MAX_RCRC_NO NUMBER := 0 ;
BEGIN
SELECT NVL( MAX(RCRD_NO) , 0)+1 INTO V_MAX_RCRC_NO FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_DTL
FNG_ACTL_CLC_DTL.STNDR_SRL = :NEW.STNDR_SRL ;
:NEW.RCRD_NO := V_MAX_RCRC_NO ;
EXCEPTION
WHEN OTHERS THEN
RAISE;
END;
CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER hrs_reduce_sgn_dplcat
ON hrs_mchn_data_attndnc
REFERENCING NEW AS NEW OLD AS OLD
PRAGMA AUTONOMOUS_TRANSACTION;
v_found NUMBER := 0;
:NEW.emp_no_mchn := :NEW.emp_no_mchn;
:NEW.emp_no := :NEW.emp_no;
:NEW.sgn_date := :NEW.sgn_date;
:NEW.sgn_time := :NEW.sgn_time;
:NEW.sgn_manual := :NEW.sgn_manual;
:NEW.mchn_ip := :NEW.mchn_ip;
:NEW.mchn_no := :NEW.MCHN_NO;
:NEW.sgn_read_flg:= :NEW.sgn_read_flg;
:NEW.ad_u_id := :NEW.ad_u_id;
:NEW.ad_date := :NEW.ad_date;
:NEW.cmp_no := :NEW.cmp_no;
:NEW.brn_no := :NEW.brn_no;
:NEW.brn_year := :NEW.brn_year;
:NEW.brn_usr := :NEW.brn_usr;
:NEW.ad_trmnl_nm := :NEW.ad_trmnl_nm;
:NEW.round_sgn_time := :NEW.round_sgn_time;
SELECT COUNT (*)
INTO v_found
WHERE emp_no_mchn = :NEW.emp_no_mchn AND sgn_time = :NEW.sgn_time /*And emp_no is not null*/ ;
:NEW.dplcat_pntr := v_found;
WHEN OTHERS
THEN
CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER CHK_HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP_TGR
BEFORE UPDATE OF WPRD_NO
ON HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP
REFERENCING NEW AS New OLD AS Old
V_COUNT NUMBER;
IF :NEW.WPRD_NO <> :OLD.WPRD_NO THEN
--CHECKING IF PREVOIUS WPRD_NO HAVE FINGER CALCULATION RECORDS ...
SELECT NVL(COUNT(*),0) INTO V_COUNT FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST M
M.WPRD_NO = :OLD.WPRD_NO
AND M.F_DATE BETWEEN :NEW.F_ACTV_DATE AND :NEW.T_ACTV_DATE ;
IF V_COUNT > 0 THEN
RAISE_APPL
ICATION_ERROR ( -20411 , 'Error In Update , It Have Calculating Records , So Cancel Calculate And then Reassign New Period ');
END IF;

"PKG INIT"PACKAGE_PRC_FNG_14_11_2016""
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE DATE_CONVERTER_PKG IS
FUNCTION GREGORIAN_TO_HIJRI(P_DATE DATE) RETURN DATE;
FUNCTION HIJRI_TO_GREGORIAN(P_DATE DATE) RETURN DATE;
END DATE_CONVERTER_PKG ;
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY DATE_CONVERTER_PKG IS
--##---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
FUNCTION INTPART(FLOATNUM FLOAT) RETURN FLOAT IS
IF (FLOATNUM< -0.0000001) THEN
RETURN CEIL(FLOATNUM-0.0000001);
RETURN FLOOR(FLOATNUM+0.0000001) ;
END INTPART ;
FUNCTION GREGORIAN_TO_HIJRI(P_DATE DATE) RETURN DATE IS
JD NUMBER;
L NUMBER;
N NUMBER;
J NUMBER;
D NUMBER:= TO_CHAR(P_DATE,'DD');
M NUMBER:= TO_CHAR(P_DATE,'MM');
Y NUMBER:= TO_CHAR(P_DATE,'YYYY');
IF P_DATE IS NULL THEN
RETURN NULL;
IF ((Y>1582)OR((Y=1582)AND(M>10))OR((Y=1582)AND(M=10)AND(D>14))) THEN
JD:=INTPART((1461*(Y+4800+INTPART((M-14)/12)))/4)+INTPART((367*(M-2-12*(INTPART((M-14)/12))))/12)-
INTPART( (3* (INTPART( (Y+4900+ INTPART( (M-14)/12) )/100) ) ) /4)+D-32075;
ELSE
JD := 367*Y-INTPART((7*(Y+5001+INTPART((M-9)/7)))/4)+INTPART((275*M)/9)+D+1729777;
L:=JD-1948440+10632;
N:=INTPART((L-1)/10631);
L:=L-10631*N+354;
J:=(INTPART((10985-L)/5316))*(INTPART((50*L)/17719))+(INTPART(L/5670))*(INTPART((43*L)/15238));
L:=L-(INTPART((30-J)/15))*(INTPART((17719*J)/50))-(INTPART(J/16))*(INTPART((15238*J)/43))+29;
M:=INTPART((24*L)/709);
D:=L-INTPART((709*M)/24);
Y:=30*N+J-30;
RETURN TO_DATE(LPAD(D,2,'0') || LPAD(M,2,'0')|| LPAD(Y,4,'0'),'DDMMYYYY');
END GREGORIAN_TO_HIJRI;
FUNCTION HIJRI_TO_GREGORIAN(P_DATE DATE) RETURN DATE IS
I NUMBER;
K NUMBER;
JD:=INTPART((11*Y+3)/30)+354*Y+30*M-INTPART((M-1)/2)+D+1948440-385;
IF (JD> 2299160 ) THEN
L:=JD+68569;
N:=INTPART((4*L)/146097);
L:=L-INTPART((146097*N+3)/4);
I:=INTPART((4000*(L+1))/1461001);
L:=L-INTPART((1461*I)/4)+31;
J:=INTPART((80*L)/2447);
D:=L-INTPART((2447*J)/80);
L:=INTPART(J/11);
M:=J+2-12*L;
Y:=100*(N-49)+I+L;
J:=JD+1402;
K:=INTPART((J-1)/1461);
L:=J-1461*K;
N:=INTPART((L-1)/365)-INTPART(L/1461);
I:=L-365*N+30;
J:=INTPART((80*I)/2447);
D:=I-INTPART((2447*J)/80);
I:=INTPART(J/11);
M:=J+2-12*I;
Y:=4*K+N+I-4716;
END HIJRI_TO_GREGORIAN ;

CURSOR PNSH_DTL (
P_PLCY_NO Fng_Plcy_CODE_MST.PLCY_NO%TYPE ,
P_ARTCL_CLASS FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.ARTCL_CLASS%TYPE
SELECT SUM (fng_v_artcl_clc.clc_val) CLC_VAL ,
SUM (fng_v_artcl_clc.PR_Clc_Val) PR_Clc_Val ,
fng_v_artcl_clc.Artcl_clss_pnsh,
DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.ARTCL_NO , fng_v_artcl_clc.artcl_no_pnsh ) Artcl_no_pnsh,
DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , fng_v_artcl_clc.FNG_TYP_FLG ) FNG_TYP_FLG,
fng_v_artcl_clc.Prd_no ,
fng_v_artcl_clc.PLCY_NO
FROM fng_v_artcl_clc , FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL
EMP_NO = P_Emp_No
AND F_DATE BETWEEN P_F_Date AND P_T_Date
AND T_DATE BETWEEN P_F_Date AND P_T_Date
AND brn_year = P_BRN_YEAR
AND brn_no = P_Brn_No
AND Artcl_clss_pnsh = FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.ARTCL_CLASS
AND DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0, FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.ARTCL_NO ) = DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0, fng_v_artcl_clc.Artcl_no_pnsh )
AND FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.PLCY_NO = P_PLCY_NO
AND FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.CLC_APLY IS NOT NULL
AND FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.CLC_LVL = 1
AND fng_v_artcl_clc.CLC_LVL = 0
GROUP BY emp_no,
fng_v_artcl_clc.artcl_clss_pnsh ,
DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.ARTCL_NO , fng_v_artcl_clc.artcl_no_pnsh ),
DECODE (FNG_PLCY_CODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , FNG_PLCY_C
ODE_DTL.FNG_TYP_FLG , fng_v_artcl_clc.FNG_TYP_FLG ) ,
fng_v_artcl_clc.hrchy_no,
fng_v_artcl_clc.plcy_no,
fng_v_artcl_clc.prd_no,
fng_v_artcl_clc.brn_year,
fng_v_artcl_clc.brn_no ,
ORDER BY ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH DESC;
FOR PRD_PRCSS IN PNSH_DTL ( I.PLCY_NO , NULL )
LOOP
F CHK_ARTCL (P_Artcl_Clss => PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH , P_FNG_TYP_FLG => PRD_PRCSS.FNG_TYP_FLG ) IS NOT NULL THEN
V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL := PRD_PRCSS.CLC_VAL ;
IF PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH = 5 THEN -- LATE PER PERIOD
IF CHK_ARTCL_TEMP (
P_Artcl_Clss => PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH ,
P_ARTCL_NO => PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_NO_PNSH ,
P_FNG_TYP_FLG => PRD_PRCSS.FNG_TYP_FLG
) = 1 THEN
--##### V_LATE_SUBSTITUTE_PER_PERIOD := PLCY_ARTCL_TEMP.LATE_SUBSTITUTE ;
--Late_Substitute ( P_Late_Type => PRD_PRCSS.FNG_TYP_FLG ) ;
Null;
IF PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH = 4 THEN
V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL := FNG_ACTL_CLC_PKG.TRANSFER_FROM_UNIT_TO_UNIT2 ( 1,2,V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL,1);
V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL
:= CLC_INFRACTION_SLICE ( P_CALCULATE_TYP => PLCY_ARTCL.CLC_SLICE_MTHD , /* CLASS OR GROUPS */
P_ARTCL_CLSS => PRD_PRCSS.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH, -- J.ARTCL_CLASS,
P_VAL => V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL -- PRD_PRCSS.CLC_VAL --* 60 -- to minute
IF V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL IS NOT NULL AND V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL > 0 THEN
UPDATE FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST D SET D.PERIOD_PNSH_UPLY = 1
WHERE D.BRN_NO = P_Brn_No
/*AND D.BRN_USR = P_Brn_Usr*/
AND D.BRN_YEAR = P_BRN_YEAR
AND D.EMP_NO = P_Emp_No
AND D.F_DATE = V_LAST_DAY;
IF PLCY_ARTCL.CLC_APLY = 1 THEN -- DELETE ARTC
L'S CALCULATED IN DAY PROCESSING ...
DEL_ARTCL_DAY_PROCESSING (
P_Brn_No => P_Brn_No ,
P_Brn_Usr => P_Brn_Usr ,
P_BRN_YEAR => P_BRN_YEAR ,
P_Emp_No => P_Emp_No ,
P_F_Date => P_F_Date ,
P_T_Date => P_T_Date ,
P_ARTCL_CLASS => PRD_PRCSS.Artcl_clss_pnsh ,
P_ARTCL_NO => PRD_PRCSS.Artcl_no_pnsh ,
ELSIF PLCY_ARTCL.CLC_APLY = 3 THEN -- DELETE ARTCL'S CALCULATED IN DAY PROCESSING ...
UPDATE_ARTCL_DAY_PROCESSING (
END LOOP;
V_ARTCL_PER_PERIOD_VAL := NULL;
V_LATE_SUBSTITUTE_PER_PERIOD := NULL;
END STRT_PERIOD_PROCESS;
/*________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________*/
DELETE FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_DTL D
D.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH = P_ARTCL_CLASS
AND DECODE ( P_FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0 , D.ARTCL_NO ) = DECODE ( P_FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0 , P_ARTCL_NO )
AND DECODE ( P_FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0 , NVL(D.FNG_TYP_FLG,0) ) = DECODE ( P_FNG_TYP_FLG , 2 , 0 , NVL(P_FNG_TYP_FLG,0) )
AND D.BRN_NO = P_Brn_No
AND D.F_DATE BETWEEN P_F_Date AND P_T_Date
AND D.T_DATE BETWEEN P_F_Date AND P_T_Date
AND D.CLC_LVL = 0 ; -- DAY PROCESSING ...
END DEL_ARTCL_DAY_PROCESSING;

P_GROUPING_TYPE IN VARCHAR2 ,
V_Qry Varchar2 (4000) ;
V_Qry :=
'Select Dm,
TO_CHAR (Dm , ''HH24:MI:SS'' ) TM ,
TO_CHAR (Dm , ''DD/MM/RRRR'' ) Dt ,
0 SGN_MANUAL,
0 Mchn_No
Into :V_Dm,:V_A_Time_In1,:V_Dt,:V_Sgn_Auto_Flg,:V_Mchn_No
From
( Select
' ||P_GROUPING_TYPE || ' ( TO_DATE( To_CHAR( Sgn_Date,''DD/MM/RRRR'') ||'' ''|| To_CHAR( Sgn_Time,''HH24:MI'') , ''DD/MM/RRRR HH24:MI'') ) Dm
From HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC
Where
/* Brn_Year ='|| P_Brn_Year || '
And Brn_No ='|| P_Brn_No || '
And Brn_Usr ='|| P_Brn_Usr || '
Emp_No ='|| P_Emp_No || '
And SGN_READ_FLG = 0
AND Sgn_Time Between TO_DATE( '''|| TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE_TIME,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS') ||''' ,''DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS'') AND TO_DATE( ''' || TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE_TIME,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS') ||''',''DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS'')
) ';
Execute Immediate V_Qry Into V_Dm,V_A_Time,V_Dt , V_Sgn_Auto_Flg,V_Mchn_No ;
IF P_RFLCT_UPD = 1 THEN
Upd_Fng_Dly_Sgntr_Prc( P_Brn_No => P_Brn_No,
P_Emp_No => P_Emp_No,
P_Mchn_No => V_Mchn_No,
P_Sgn_Time => Chrtime_Fnc( V_Dt , V_A_Time )
Exception When OTHERS Then
V_A_Time := Null;
V_Dt := Null;
V_Dm := Null;
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'TIME_PRCSS >> Error '||sqlerrm || ' '||V_Qry );
END TIME_PRCSS;

Function Get_Plcy_Pnsh_Fnc (
P_Plcy_No Fng_Plcy_Code_Mst.Plcy_No%Type,
P_Artcl_Clss Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl.Artcl_Clss%Type,
P_Infrctn_Typ Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl.Infrctn_Typ%Type
) Return Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl%Rowtype
SELECT * /* ALL COLUMNS */
Into V_Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl
From Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl M
M.Plcy_No = P_Plcy_No
And M.Artcl_Clss = Artcl_Clss
And M.Infrctn_Typ = P_Infrctn_Typ
AND M.TIME_AP
LY = DECODE (V_RST_PNTR , 0 , 1 , 2 ) ;
Return V_Fng_Plcy_Pnsh_Dtl;
Exception When Others Then
End Get_Plcy_Pnsh_Fnc;
PROCEDURE IS_CLC_ADDITIONAL_TIMES (P_DATE DATE ) IS
--________________________________________________________________
SELECT NVL(HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL.INCRS_MNT_TIME,0) INTO V_INCRS_MNT_TIME /* SET INTO GLOBAL VARIABLE */
FROM HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL
WHERE HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL.WPRD_NO = I.WPRD_NO
AND HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => P_DATE ) ) ) ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
V_INCRS_MNT_TIME := 0 ; /* RESET GLOBAL VARIABLE */
END ;
Function Chrtime_Fnc(P_Date Date, P_Ctime VARCHAR2 , P_MINUTE NUMBER DEFAULT NULL ) Return DATE Is
V_Time Date;
V_HOUR NUMBER := 0;
V_MINUTE NUMBER := 0 ;
IF P_MINUTE IS NULL THEN
If P_Ctime Is Not Null Then
Select To_Date (To_Char(P_Date,'DD/MM/RRRR')||' '|| P_Ctime ,'DD/MM/RRRR HH24:MI:SS') Into V_Time From Dual;
Else
V_Time := Null;
End If;
ELSIF P_MINUTE IS NOT NULL THEN
V_HOUR := TRUNC (P_MINUTE/60) ;
V_MINUTE := MOD (P_MINUTE,60) ;
Select To_Date (To_Char(P_Date,'DD/MM/RRRR')||' '|| P_Ctime ,'DD/MM/RRRR HH24:MI:SS') + V_HOUR/24 + V_MINUTE/(60*24) Into V_Time From Dual;
Return V_Time ;
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'Chrtime_Fnc >> erorrrrrrrrrrr '||SQLERRM || P_Date ||' >> '|| P_Ctime ||' >> '||P_MINUTE);
End Chrtime_Fnc;
/*_________________________________________________________________________
___________________________________________________________*/
P_Stndr_Srl Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.Stndr_Srl%Type) Return FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST%Rowtype
V_Fng_Stndr_Clc FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST%Rowtype;
SELECT * /* ALL */
Into V_Fng_Stndr_Clc
From FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST
Where Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.Brn_No = P_Brn_No
And Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.Stndr_Srl = P_Stndr_Srl ;
Return V_Fng_Stndr_Clc;
End Get_Stnd_Data_Fnc;
V_Sgn_Date_TIME DATE ;
V_Sgn_Date_TIME2 DATE ;
If P_Sgn_Time Is Not Null Then
Update HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC
Set SGN_READ_FLG = 1,
WPRD_NO = I.WPRD_NO ,
PRD_NO = I.PRD_NO
Where Brn_No = P_Brn_No
And TO_CHAR(ROUND_Sgn_Time,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS') = TO_CHAR(P_Sgn_Time,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS')
And Emp_No = P_Emp_No
/*And NVL(Mchn_No ,0) = NVL(P_Mchn_No , 0)*/
And Rownum<=1 ;
Elsif P_Sgn_Date_TIME2 Is Not Null And P_Sgn_Date_TIME3 Is Not Null Then
V_Sgn_Date_TIME := TO_DATE(P_Sgn_Date_TIME2,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS') ;
V_Sgn_Date_TIME2 := TO_DATE(P_Sgn_Date_TIME3,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI:SS') ;
date HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC
Set SGN_READ_FLG = 1 , -- Open Period
/*And NVL(Mchn_No ,0) = NVL(P_Mchn_No , 0) */
And Chrtime_Fnc(Sgn_Date , Sgn_Time) Between P_Sgn_Date_TIME2 AND P_Sgn_Date_TIME3;
End Upd_Fng_Dly_Sgntr_Prc;
FUNCTION CHK_HLDY_TSK_PRMSON ( P_TRANS_TYP NUMBER , P_TYP NUMBER DEFAULT NULL , P_TIME_CHK DATE DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER
V_CNT NUMBER:= 0 ;
IF P_TYP = 1 THEN
-- CHECKING IF EMPLOYEE HAVE HOLIDAY IN THIS PERIOD FOR WHOLE TIME
COUNT(*) INTO V_CNT
FROM FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL WHERE
FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.STNDR_SRL = I.STNDR_SRL
AND FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.TRANS_TYP = P_TRANS_TYP
AND NVL(FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.DUR,0) = 0
AND ROWNUM = 1 ;
RETURN V_CNT ;
ELSIF P_TYP = 2 THEN
-- CHECKING IF EMPLOYEE HAVE TASK IN THIS PERIOD FOR Hours TIME
AND FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.FNG_RQD = 0
AND P_TIME_CHK BETWEEN Chrtime_Fnc( P_TIME_CHK , TIME_IN) AND Chrtime_Fnc( P_TIME_CHK , TIME_OUT) ;
--CHECKING IF EMPLOYEE HAVE HOLIDAY OR TASK PER HOURS IN WORKING PERIOD
EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN RETURN V_CNT ;
V_PERIOD_FACTOR NUMBER ;
IF SIGN(P_CLC_VAL) <> 0 OR P_CLC_VAL > 0 THEN
V_PERIOD_FACTOR := -- CAPTURE RETURN FATCR ...
GET_EMP_PERIOD_FACTOR (
P_ARTCL_NO => INFRCTION.ARTCL_NO_PNSH ,
P_Emp_No => I.Emp_No ,
P_CLC_Date => I.F_Date ,
P_WPRD_NO => I.Wprd_No
Insert Into Fng_Actl_Clc_DTL(
STNDR_SRL,
EMP_NO,
PLCY_NO,
WPRD_NO,
F_DATE,
T_DATE,
ARTCL_CLSS,
ARTCL_NO,
F_VAL,
T_VAL,
PNSH_VAL,
A_VAL,
PR_CLC_VAL,
CLC_VAL,
OVR_PLS_VAL,
INFRCTN_TYP,
ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH ,
ARTCL_NO_PNSH ,
FNG_TYP_FLG ,
CLC_SLICE_MTHD,
CLC_LVL ,
BRN_YEAR,
BRN_NO,
BRN_USR,
AD_U_ID ,
AD_DATE ,
TIME_APLY ,
PRD_NO ,
CMP_NO ,
PERIOD_FACTOR
Values (
I.Stndr_Srl,
I.Emp_No ,
I.Plcy_No ,
I.Wprd_No ,
I.F_Date , I.T_Date ,
INFRCTION.ARTCL_CLSS,
INFRCTION.ARTCL_NO_pnsh, --INFRCTION.ARTCL_NO 22/10/2015
INFRCTION.F_VAL,
INFRCTION.T_VAL,
INFRCTION.PNSH_VAL ,
NULL,
NVL(P_PR_CLC_VAL,0),
NVL(P_CLC_VAL ,0) ,
NVL(P_OVR_PLS_VAL,0),
INFRCTION.INFRCTN_TYP,
INFRCTION.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH ,
INFRCTION.ARTCL_NO_PNSH ,
INFRCTION.FNG_TYP_FLG ,
P_CLC_SLICE_MTHD,
P_CLC_LVL ,
I.Brn_Year ,
I.Brn_No ,
I.Brn_Usr ,
V_Crt_Usr ,
SYSDATE ,
DECODE (V_RST_PNTR , 0 , 1 , 2),
I.PRD_NO ,
IAS_Brn_Pkg.Get_Br_Cmp ( G_BRN_NO => I.Brn_No ) ,
V_PERIOD_FACTOR
-- COMMIT;
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'Insrt_Fng_Act_Clc_Gud_Dtl_Prc ^^ ERROR '||SQLERRM );
UPDATE Fng_Actl_Clc_DTL SET
PR_CLC_VAL = PR_CLC_VAL ,
CLC_VAL = CLC_VAL + p_clc_val -- 22/12/2015
EMP_NO = I.Emp_No AND
PRD_NO = I.PRD_NO AND
INFRCTN_TYP = INFRCTION.INFRCTN_TYP AND
ARTCL_CLSS = INFRCTION.ARTCL_CLSS_PNSH AND
ARTCL_NO = INFRCTION.ARTCL_NO_PNSH AND
F_DATE = I.F_Date AND
T_DATE = I.T_Date AND
WPRD_NO = I.Wprd_No AND
PLCY_NO = I.Plcy_No ;
END Insrt_Fng_Act_Clc_Gud_Dtl_Prc;
P_S_Add_Totl Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.S_Add_Totl%Type ,
P_HLDY_FLG Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.HLDY_FLG%Type,
P_OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG%TYPE , -- 24/08/2015 ALMANSOUR
P_DAY_OFF_FLG Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.DAY_OFF_FLG%TYPE -- 24/08/2015 ALMANSOUR
V_ASSGN_TIME_IN
Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.A_Time_In%Type ;
V_ASSGN_TIME_OUT Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.A_Time_Out%Type ;
V_TIME_IN Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.A_Time_In%Type ;
V_TIME_OUT Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst.A_Time_Out%Type ;
V_ASSGN_DUR FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST.ASSGN_DUR%TYPE;
IF I.ASSGN_FLG IS NOT NULL THEN
V_ASSGN_TIME_IN := P_A_Time_In ;
V_ASSGN_TIME_OUT := P_A_Time_Out;
V_ASSGN_DUR:= P_A_Day_Hours ;
V_TIME_IN := P_A_Time_In ;
V_TIME_OUT := P_A_Time_Out;
Insert Into Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst(Stndr_Srl ,
Emp_No ,
Plcy_No ,
Wprd_No ,
F_Date ,
T_Date ,
