AND DOC_SRL IN ( SELECT DOC_SRL FROM HRS_ADD_ASGN_MST
ng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PRCSS_TSK_PERMMSION_HLDY ^^ ERROR ^^' || SQLERRM );
END PRCSS_TSK_PERMMSION_HLDY;
FUNCTION GET_ROUND_TIME (P_DATE DATE , P_INTERVAL NUMBER, P_ROUND NUMBER ) RETURN DATE
V_MOD NUMBER ;
V_DIV NUMBER ;
V_HOUR NUMBER := to_char(P_DATE,'HH24') ;
V_MINUTE NUMBER := to_char(P_DATE,'MI') ;
V_ROUND_MINUTE NUMBER ;
V_ROUND_HOUR NUMBER ;
IF P_INTERVAL <> 0 OR P_INTERVAL IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT MOD (V_MINUTE,P_INTERVAL) ,
FLOOR (V_MINUTE/P_INTERVAL)
INTO V_MOD , V_DIV
FROM DUAL ;
IF V_MOD >= P_ROUND THEN
V_ROUND_MINUTE := ( V_DIV * P_INTERVAL ) + P_INTERVAL ;
V_ROUND_MINUTE := ( V_DIV * P_INTERVAL ) ;
IF V_ROUND_MINUTE >= 60 THEN
V_ROUND_MINUTE := 0 ;
V_ROUND_HOUR := 60;
RETURN Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( P_DATE , TO_CHAR(V_HOUR||':'||V_ROUND_MINUTE) , V_ROUND_HOUR ) ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN P_DATE ;
PROCEDURE RFLCT_ROUND_TIME ( P_EMP_NO S_EMP.EMP_NO%TYPE , P_PLCY_NO FNG_PLCY_CODE_MST.PLCY_NO%TYPE , P_F_DATE DATE , P_T_DATE DATE ) IS
CURSOR GET_SIGNATURE IS
SELECT * FROM HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC WHERE EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO AND SGN_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE ;--FOR UPDATE;
SIGNATURE_CUR HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC%ROWTYPE ;
V_ROUND_DATE DATE ;
V_MINUTE_ROUND FNG_PARA.ROUND_FINGER_TIME%TYPE;
V_INTERVAL FNG_PARA.ROUND_FINGER_STRT_TIME%TYPE;
SELECT ROUND_FINGER_TIME , ROUND_FINGER_STRT_TIME INTO
V_MINUTE_ROUND , V_INTERVAL
FROM FNG_PLCY_CODE_MST WHERE FNG_PLCY_CODE_MST.PLCY_NO = P_PLCY_NO ;
IF V_MINUTE_ROUND IS NULL OR V_INTERVAL IS NULL THEN
FROM FNG_PARA WHERE ROWNUM=1 ;
EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
V_MINUTE_ROUND := 1 ;
V_INTERVAL := 1 ;
FOR SIGNATURE_CUR IN GET_SIGNATURE LOOP
V_ROUND_DATE := GET_ROUND_TIME (P_DATE => SIGNATURE_CUR.SGN_TIME , P_INTERVAL =>V_INTERVAL, P_ROUND => V_MINUTE_ROUND ) ;
UPDATE HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC SET ROUND_SGN_TIME = V_ROUND_DATE
WHERE HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND TO_CHAR(HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.SGN_TIME,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI') = TO_CHAR(SIGNATURE_CUR.SGN_TIME,'DD-MM-RRRR HH24:MI') ;
FUNCTION GET_DAY_OFF_DATES2 ( P_Sprdt DATE ,
RETURN FNG_TYP_LST_DATES_SET PIPELINED AS
OUT_REC FNG_TYP_LST_DATES := FNG_TYP_LST_DATES (NULL,NULL,NULL);
V_PRV_ABSNT NUMBER ;
V_PRV_ABSNT_DATE DATE ;
V_NXT_ABSNT NUMBER ;
V_NXT_ABSNT_DATE DATE ;
For L In (Select Dat,Day_No ,RowNum RowN
umber
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PRAGMA AUTONOMOUS_TRANSACTION ;
V_F_INFRCTION_TYP NUMBER ;
V_T_INFRCTION_TYP NUMBER ;
FOR L IN
SELECT dat , day_no , rownumber , S.ABSNT_FLG PERIOD_DAY_ABSENT , DECODE ( s.F_DATE , NULL , 0, DECODE(S.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,1,0) ) DAY_WORK , DECODE ( Dat , s.F_DATE , 0 , 1) DAY_OFF_WORK
TABLE ( Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.GET_DAY_OFF_DATES2 ( P_Sprdt => P_Sprdt,
P_Eprdt => P_Eprdt
) ) left outer join FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST S on (
Dat = S.F_DATE
AND S.BRN_NO = P_Brn_No
/*AND S.BRN_USR = P_BRN_Usr */
S.WPRD_NO
= I.WPRD_NO
AND S.BRN_YEAR = P_Brn_Year
AND (NVL(S.HLDY_FLG,0) = 1 OR NVL(S.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) = 1 )
-- AND S.Day_off_flg = 1
WHERE (NVL(S.HLDY_FLG,0) = 1 OR NVL(S.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) = 1 )
FDATE ,
(SELECT DECODE(N.ABSNT
_FLG,NULL,DECODE ( N.HLDY_FLG , 0 , NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) ,N.HLDY_FLG ) ,2) FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
Where N.F_DATE = FDATE AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO )
INTO V_PRV_ABSNT_DATE , V_PRV_ABSNT
MAX(F_DATE) FDATE
FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
To_Date(N.F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') < To_Date(L.DAT,'DD-MM-RRRR')
AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND NVL(N.DAY_OFF_FLG,0) = 0
/*SELECT
DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,DECODE ( N.HLDY_FLG , 0 , NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) ,N.HLDY_FLG ) ,2)
FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N ,
N.WPRD_NO
Where N.F_DATE = M.FDATE
AND NVL(N.INFRCTN_TYP,0) = 0
= I.WPRD_NO;
DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,DECODE ( N.HLDY_FLG , 0 , NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) ,N.HLDY_FLG ) , DECODE (N.INFRCTN_TYP ,0,2,9) )
AND NVL(N.DAY_OFF_FLG,0) = 0 AND NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) = 0
--AND N.INFRCTN_TYP = 0
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_PRV_ABSNT := 0 ;
NVL((SELECT
DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,DECODE ( N.HLDY_FLG , 0 , NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) ,N.HLDY_FLG ) ,2) FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
Where N.F_DATE = FDATE AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO ) ,1 )
INTO V_NXT_ABSNT_DATE , V_NXT_ABSNT
MIN(F_DATE) FDATE
To_Date(N.F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') > To_Date(L.DAT,'DD-MM-RRRR')
DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,DECODE ( N.HLDY_FLG , 0 , NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) ,N.HLDY_FLG ) ,DECODE (N.INFRCTN_TYP ,0,2,9) )
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_NXT_ABSNT := 1 ;
IF (V_PRV_ABSNT = 2 AND V_NXT_ABSNT = 2 AND FNG_PRMTR.ABSNT_DAY_OFF_CALC = 1 )
OR ((V_PRV_ABSNT = 2 AND V_NXT_ABSNT = 1) AND FNG_PRMTR.ABSNT_DAY_OFF_CALC2 = 1 )
OR ((V_PRV_ABSNT = 1 AND V_NXT_ABSNT = 2) AND FNG_PRMTR.ABSNT_DAY_OFF_CALC2 = 1 )
(SELECT DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,0,N.ABSNT_FLG) FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
Where N.F_DATE = FDATE AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO AND (NVL(N.HLDY_FLG,0) <> 1 OR NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) <> 1 ) ) ,
(SELECT N.INFRCTN_TYP FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
Where N.F_DATE = FDATE AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO AND (NVL(N.HLDY_FLG,0) <> 1 OR NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) <> 1 )
INTO V_PRV_ABSNT_DATE , V_PRV_ABSNT , V_F_INFRCTION_TYP
--AND N.STNDR_DAY_HOURS <> 0
--AND NVL(N.HLDY_FLG,0) <> 1
NVL((SELECT DECODE(N.ABSNT_FLG,NULL,0,N.ABSNT_FLG) FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST N
Where N.F_DATE = FDATE AND N.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO AND (NVL(N.HLDY_FLG,0) <> 1 OR NVL(N.OFFICIAL_HLDY_FLG ,0) <> 1 ) ),1) ,
INTO V_NXT_ABSNT_DATE , V_NXT_ABSNT , V_T_INFRCTION_TYP
To_Date(N.F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') > To_Date(L.DAT,'DD-MM
-RRRR')
IF (V_PRV_ABSNT = 1 AND V_NXT_ABSNT = 1 )
--AND (V_NXT_ABSNT_DATE IS NOT NULL AND V_PRV_ABSNT_DATE IS NOT NULL )
--AND (NVL(V_F_INFRCTION_TYP,0) = 0 AND NVL(V_T_INFRCTION_TYP,0) = 0 )
END GET_DAY_OFF_DATES;
FUNCTION CHK_ARTCL_DAY_OFF RETURN HRS_ARTCL.ARTCL_NO%TYPE IS
V_ARTCL_NO HRS_ARTCL.ARTCL_NO%TYPE := NULL;
H.ARTCL_NO INTO V_ARTCL_NO
H.ARTCL_RSRVD_NO = 17 /* DEFINED DAY OFF ABSENT PROCESSING */
AND NVL(INACTIVE , 0 ) = 0 /* ARTCL IS ACTIVE */
AND NVL(H.FNG_FLG,0) = 1
AND H.ARTCL_CLASS = 4 ; -- ABSENT
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN V_ARTCL_NO;
CURSOR GET_DAY_OFF IS
Dat , P_PLCY_NO , P_WPRD_NO FROM
TABLE ( Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.GET_DAY_OFF_DATES ( P_Sprdt => P_Sprdt ,
P_Eprdt => P_Eprdt ,
P_BRN_Usr => P_BRN_Usr ,
P_EMP_NO => P_EMP_NO
V_Sr Number ;
V_STNDR_SRL Number ;
WPRD_DAY_HOUR NUMBER ;
V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF NUMBER ;
CURSOR CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc (P_DAY_OFF DATE )IS
From FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST M
AND M.WPRD_NO = P_WPRD_NO
AND M.F_DATE = P_DAY_OFF
AND M.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND M.PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO ;
Y FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST%ROWTYPE ;
IF FNG_PRMTR.ABSNT_DAY_OFF_CALC = 0 AND FNG_PRMTR.ABSNT_DAY_OFF_CALC2 = 0 THEN
V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF := CHK_ARTCL_DAY_OFF ;
IF V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF IS NOT NULL THEN
Select Nvl((Max(Stndr_Srl))+1,1) Into V_Sr From Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST;
FOR DAY_OFF IN GET_DAY_OFF LOOP
OPEN CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc (P_DAY_OFF => DAY_OFF.DAT ) ;
FETCH CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc INTO Y ;
IF Y.STNDR_SRL IS NULL THEN
Insert Into Fng_Actl_Clc_Mst(
Stndr_Srl ,
DOC_TYP ,
HRCHY_NO ,
Stndr_day_hours
Values ( V_SR ,
90 ,
P_Emp_No ,
P_Plcy_No ,
P_Wprd_No ,
DAY_OFF.DAT ,
P_Brn_Year ,
P_Brn_No ,
P_Crt_Usr ,
P_Trmnl ,
ys_emp_pkg.get_emp_fld_val (p_fld_nm => 'HRCHY_NO',p_emp_no => P_emp_no ) ,
P_PRD_NO ,
IAS_Brn_Pkg.Get_Br_Cmp ( G_BRN_NO => P_Brn_No ) ,
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PRCESS_CONTIONOUS_ABSENT ^^ERROR^^ '||SQLERRM );
PERIOD_FACTOR ,
ABSENT_TYP
V_SR ,
4 , -- ABSENT CLASS
V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF , -- DEFINED DAY OFF ARTCL FROM HRS ARTCL
1 ,-- Implement One Day --V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ,
1 ,-- Implement One Day -- V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ,
4, -- ABSENT CLASS
V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF ,-- DEFINED DAY OFF ARTCL FROM HRS ARTCL
V_PERIOD_FACTOR ,
V_Sr := V_Sr + 1 ;
V_PERIOD_FACTOR :=
P_ARTCL_NO => V_ARTCL_DAY_OFF , -- ABSENT DAY OFF ARTCL
P_Emp_No => Y.Emp_No ,
P_CLC_Date => Y.F_DATE ,
P_WPRD_NO => Y.Wprd_No
V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR := GET_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ( P_WPRD_NO => Y.WPRD_NO ,
P_CLC_Date => DAY_OFF.DAT ) ;
Y.STNDR_SRL ,
Y.Emp_No ,
Y.Plcy_No ,
Y.Wprd_No ,
Y.F_DATE ,
Y.T_DATE ,
Y.STNDR_DAY_HOURS ,-- Implement One Day --V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ,
Y.STNDR_DAY_HOURS ,-- Implement One Day -- V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ,
4, -- ABSENT
CLASS
2 -- DAY OFF
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PRCESS_CONTIONOUS_ABSENT ^^ERROR^^'||SQLERRM );
END PRCESS_CONTIONOUS_ABSENT;
FUNCTION CHK_ARTCL_REFUND RETURN HRS_ARTCL.ARTCL_NO%TYPE IS
H.ARTCL_RSRVD_NO = 14 /* DEFINED REFUND FROM ABSENT */
PROCEDURE ARTCL_REFUND IS
V_ARTCL_DOC_TYP VARCHAR2(200);
V_ARTCL_DOC_SRL VARCHAR2(200);
V_ROWNUM NUMBER := 1 ;
V_DESC VARCHAR2(4000);
V_ARTCL_REFUND HRS_ARTCL.ARTCL_NO%TYPE ;
V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR NUMBER := GET_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ( P_WPRD_NO => I.WPRD_NO , P_CLC_Date => I.F_DATE ) ;
V_ARTCL_REFUND := CHK_ARTCL_REFUND ;
IF V_ARTCL_REFUND IS NOT NULL THEN /* ARTCL REFUND IS FOUND */
FOR ARTCL_REFUND IN (
ABS(HLDY_BLNC - ADJST_HLDY_BLNC) Clc_val ,
V_ARTCL_REFUND Artcl_no_pnsh
FROM HRS_HLDY_ADJST H
BRN_NO = I.BRN_NO
AND BRN_YEAR = I.BRN_YEAR
AND ADJST_TYP = 3 -- REFUND FROM ABSENT
AND PRD_NO_HRS = I.PRD_NO
And YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val ('HRCHY_NO', H.EMP_NO) = NVL(I.HRCHY_NO , YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val ('HRCHY_NO', H.EMP_NO))
AND EMP_NO = I.EMP_NO
)LOOP
4 , -- ABSENT REFUND
V_ARTCL_REFUND , -- RELATED ARTCLE FOR ABSENT REFUND
ARTCL_REFUND.CLC_VAL , -- * V_WPRD_DAY_HOUR ,
EMP_FNG_CALC NUMBER ;
EMP_COUNT NUMBER ;
V_FNG_CALC NUMBER ;
SELECT COUNT(DECODE ( FNG_CALC , 0 , NULL , 1 )) EMP_FNG_CALC , COUNT (EMP_NO_MCHN) EMP_COUNT
INTO EMP_FNG_CALC , EMP_COUNT
FROM S_EMP
WHERE EMP_NO_MCHN IS NOT NULL
AND FNG_CALC IS NOT NULL
AND BRN_NO = P_BRN_NO ;
IF EMP_FNG_CALC <> EMP_COUNT THEN
V_FNG_CALC := 0 ; -- MEAN NOT ALL EMPLOYEE HAVE PROCESS ATTANDANCE
ELSIF EMP_FNG_CALC <> EMP_COUNT THEN
V_FNG_CALC := 1 ; -- MEAN ALL EMPLOYEE HAVE PROCESS ATTANDANCE
UPDATE HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS
FNG_CALC = V_FNG_CALC ,
FNG_CALC_CNT = FNG_CALC_CNT +1 ,
FNG_CALC_DATE = SYSDATE ,
FNG_CALC_U_ID = P_USR_NO
PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO
AND BRN_NO = P_BRN_NO;
FUNCTION CHK_EMP_FNG_TRANS
P_WPRD_NO FNG_ACT
L_CLC_MST.WPRD_NO%TYPE
V_FNG_MSG NUMBER := 0 ;
V_CNT NUMBER ;
IF P_TRANS_DATE IS NOT NULL AND P_BRN_NO IS NOT NULL AND P_EMP_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT COUNT(*) INTO V_CNT
WHERE HRS_APRV <> 1
AND EMP_NO = NVL(P_EMP_NO , EMP_NO )
AND BRN_NO = P_BRN_NO
AND WPRD_NO = NVL(P_WPRD_NO , WPRD_NO)
HAVING
P_TRANS_DATE BETWEEN MIN(FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST.F_DATE) AND MAX(FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST.F_DATE) ;
IF V_CNT > 0 THEN
V_FNG_MSG := 2278 ; -- 2029 ;
Return V_FNG_MSG ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN Return 0 ;
END CHK_EMP_FNG_TRANS;

P_Hrchy_No => YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val (P_Fld_Nm => 'HRCHY_NO' ,P_EMP_NO => P_EMP_NO ) ,
P_EMP_JOB_NO => YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val (P_Fld_Nm => 'EMP_JOB_NO',P_EMP_NO => P_EMP_NO ) ,
P_Wprd_No => NULL ,
P_Artcl_No => P_ARTCL_NO,
P_Doc_Val => P_Artcl_Val ,
P_Dur_Day => NULL,
P_Ac_Code => P_Ac_Code ,
P_Ac_Code_Dtl => Null ,
P_Cur_Code => P_Cur_Code ,
P_Cc_Code => Null ,
P_Pj_No => Null ,
P_Actv_No => Null ,
P_Instlmnt_No => Null ,
P_Approved => NULL,
P_Aprv_U_Id => NULL,
P_Aprv_Date => NULL,
P_Aprv_Dsc => Null,
P_Fill_Typ => Null ,
P_Doc_No_Ref => Null ,
P_Doc_Typ_Ref => Null ,
P_Doc_Srl_Ref => Null ,
P_Prd_No => P_Prd_No_Hrs,
P_Yr_No => P_Yr_No_Hrs ,
P_Ad_U_id => P_Ad_U_id ,
P_Add_Date => P_Ad_Date ,
P_Up_U_Id => P_Up_U_Id ,
P_Up_Date => P_Up_Date ,
P_Up_Cnt => P_Up_Cnt ,
P_Pr_Rep => Null ,
P_Cmp_No => P_Cmp_No ,
P_EFFCT_HLDY_BLNC => 1
Raise_Application_Error (-20020,'Error When INSERTING ARTCL_MOV_DTL '|| CHR (13) || SQLERRM);
END Insrt_Artcl_Mov_Dtl;
P_PRD_NO_HRS In Hrs_Artcl
_Mov_Dtl.PRD_NO_HRS%TYPE,
P_Brn_No In Hrs_Artcl_Mov_Dtl.BRN_NO%TYPE
) Is
Delete From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_RQ_Dtl
AND YR_NO_HRS = P_Brn_Year
AND PRD_NO_HRS = P_PRD_NO_HRS
AND BRN_NO = P_Brn_No
AND DOC_SQ_REF = 790 ; -- FINGER CALCULATE TYPE
Delete From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_RQ_Mst
YR_NO_HRS = P_Brn_Year
AND DOC_TYP_REF = 790 -- FINGER CALCULATE TYPE
AND (Doc_SRL)
IN (
SELECT Doc_SRL
SELECT M.DOC_NO ,M.Doc_SRL,M.PRD_NO_HRS ,M.YR_NO_HRS ,M.BRN_NO ,
(SELECT COUNT(*) From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_RQ_DTL D WHERE PRCSSD_FLG = 0 AND APPROVED = 0 AND D.DOC_NO =M.DOC_NO AND D.Doc_SRL = M.Doc_SRL AND D.PRD_NO_HRS = M.PRD_NO_HRS AND D.YR_NO_HRS = M.YR_NO_HRS AND D.BRN_NO = M.BRN_NO ) DTL_ZERO
From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_RQ_MST M
) WHERE
DTL_ZERO = 0
Delete From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_Dtl
Delete From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_Mst
(SELECT COUNT(*) From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_DTL D WHERE /* PRCSSD_FLG = 0 AND APPROVED = 0 */ D.DOC_NO =M.DOC_NO AND D.Doc_SRL = M.Doc_SRL AND D.PRD_NO_HRS = M.PRD_NO_HRS AND D.YR_NO_HRS = M.YR_NO_HRS AND D.BRN_NO = M.BRN_NO ) DTL_ZERO
From Hrs_Artcl_Mov_MST M
--REMOVE EMPLOYEE MOVMENT
FOR I IN ( SELECT DISTINCT ARTCL_DOC_SRL FROM FNG_ACTL_CLC_MST
PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO_HRS
AND EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO )
HRS_TRNS_PKG.Delete_Emp_Movmnt_Prc ( P_Doc_Typ => TO_NUMBER(SUBSTR(I.ARTCL_DOC_SRL , 8 ,5 )) ,
P_Doc_Srl => I.ARTCL_DOC_SRL,
P_Doc_Sq => NULL );
END Delete_Artcl_Mov_Prc;
FUNCTION GET_Hrs_Artcl_DOC_NO(P_VAR VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER IS
IF P_VAR = 'DOC_SRL' THEN
RETURN G_DOC_SRL ;
RETURN G_DOC_NO ;
V_VAL VARCHAR2(10) ;
V_HOUR VARCHAR2(10) ;
V_MIN VARCHAR2(10) ;
V_VAL := TO_CHAR(P_VAL) ;
V_HOUR := SUBSTR( V_VAL , 1,INSTR(V_VAL,'.')-1) ;
IF SUBSTR( V_VAL , INSTR(V_VAL,'.')+1) > 59 Then
V_MIN := SUBSTR( V_VAL , INSTR(V_VAL,'.')+1) - 60 ;
V_HOUR := TO_NUMBER(V_HOUR) + 1 ;
RETURN V_HOUR||'.'||V_MIN ;
RETURN P_VAL ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN P_VAL ;
V_UNIT_VAL VARCHAR2(200):= P_UNIT_VAL ;
V_HOUR NUMBER ;
V_MIN NUMBER ;
V_TOT_MIN NUMBER ;
-- __________________________________________
IF P_F_UNIT = 1 /* FROM HOURE */ AND P_T_UNIT = 2 THEN /* TO MINUTE */
IF INSTR(TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , '.') <> 0 THEN -- FOUND FRACTION IN NUMBER ....
-- EXTRACT HOUR & MINUTE
V_HOUR := NVL(SUBSTR( TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , 1 , INSTR(TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , '.') -1 ),0) ;
V_MIN := NVL(SUBSTR( TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , INSTR(TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , '.') +1 ),0) ;
IF LENGTH(V_MIN) = 1 THEN
V_MIN := TO_NUMBER(V_MIN||'0') ;
V_TOT_MIN := ((V_HOUR * 60 ) + V_MIN) * P_PNSH_VAL ;
RETURN V_TOT_MIN ;
ELSIF INSTR(TO_CHAR(P_UNIT_VAL) , '.') = 0 THEN -- FOUND FRACTION IN NUMBER ....
V_HOUR := P_UNIT_VAL ;
V_TOT_MIN := ((V_HOUR * 60 ) ) * P_PNSH_VAL ;
ELSIF P_F_UNIT = 2 /* TO MINUTE */ AND P_T_UNIT = 1 /* FROM HOURE */ THEN
--V_HOUR := TO_NUMBER (TRUNC( P_UNIT_VAL/60) ||'.'||LPAD(CEIL (MOD( P_UNIT_VAL/60,1) * 60 ),2,0 )) ;
V_HOUR := TRUNC(P_UNIT_VAL/60) + ( MOD( P_UNIT_VAL/60,1) * 60 )/100 ;
RETURN V_HOUR ;
RETURN P_UNIT_VAL ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN P_UNIT_VAL ;
END TRANSFER_FROM_UNIT_TO_UNIT2;
V_SIGNATURES VARCHAR2(4000):= NULL ;
CURSOR GET_SIGNATURES (
P_PERIOD_STRT DATE DEFAULT P_F_DATE ,
P_PERIOD_END DATE DEFAULT P_T_DATE ,
P_INNER_PERIOD_STRT DATE DEFAULT P_F_DATE ,
P_INNER_PER
IOD_END DATE DEFAULT P_T_DATE
SELECT DISTINCT TO_CHAR(HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.SGN_TIME,'HH24:MI') SGN_TIME FROM HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC
HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.SGN_TIME NOT BETWEEN P_PERIOD_STRT AND P_PERIOD_END
AND HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.SGN_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE
AND HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.SGN_READ_FLG = 0
AND HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.BRN_NO = P_Brn_No
AND HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.BRN_NO = P_Brn_No ;
V_PERIOD_STRT DATE ;
V_PERIOD_END DATE ;
V_INNER_PERIOD_STRT DATE ;
V_INNER_PERIOD_END DATE ;
IF P_WPRD_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
--________________________________________________________________________________________
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( P_F_Date , TO_CHAR( NVL(H.F_TIME_IN ,H.TIME_IN),'HH24:MI') ) ,
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( P_F_Date , TO_CHAR( NVL(H.T_TIME_IN ,H.TIME_IN),'HH24:MI') ,1 )
INTO V_PERIOD_STRT , V_INNER_PERIOD_STRT
AND H.DAY_ST = 1
AND H.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => To_Char( To_Date( P_F_Date,'DD-MM-RRRR') ,'D') ) );
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( P_T_Date , TO_CHAR( NVL(H.T_TIME_OUT ,H.TIME_OUT) ,'HH24:MI') ) ,
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( P_T_Date , TO_CHAR( NVL(H.F_TIME_OUT ,H.TIME_OUT) ,'HH24:MI') ,-1 )
INTO V_PERIOD_END , V_INNER_PERIOD_END
AND H.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => To_Char( To_Date( P_T_Date,'DD-MM-RRRR') ,'D') ) );
FOR I IN GET_SIGNATURES (
P_PERIOD_STRT => V_PERIOD_STRT ,
P_PERIOD_END => V_PERIOD_END ,
P_INNER_PERIOD_STRT => V_INNER_PERIOD_STRT ,
P_INNER_PERIOD_END => V_INNER_PERIOD_END
IF V_SIGNATURES IS NOT NULL THEN
V_SIGNATURES := V_SIGNATURES || ',' || I.SGN_TIME ;
V_SIGNATURES := I.SGN_TIME ;
RETURN V_SIGNATURES ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS
THEN RETURN
NULL;
END GET_EMP_SIGNATURE_OUT_PERIOD;

FUNCTION GET_TOT_TSK_PRMSN RETURN NUMBER
V_TOT NUMBER ;
SELECT SUM(FNG_ACTL_CLC_PKG.TRANSFER_FROM_UNIT_TO_UNIT2 (1,2,DUR,1))
INTO V_TOT
FROM FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL
FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.STNDR_SRL IN (SELECT STNDR_SRL FROM FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST WHERE EMP_NO = I.EMP_NO AND F_DATE = I.F_DATE AND T_DATE = I.T_DATE AND WPRD_NO = I.WPRD_NO )
AND FNG_STNDR_CLC_DTL.TRANS_TYP IN (1,2);
FNG_ACTL_CLC_PKG.TRANSFER_FROM_UNIT_TO_UNIT2 (2,1,V_TOT,1);
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN 0 ;
END GET_TOT_TSK_PRMSN;
PROCEDURE NO_SINGNATURE_FOUND IS
IF (V_TSK_FLG IS NULL AND V_PRMSN_FLG IS NULL AND V_HLDY_FLG IS NULL) --OR (V_A_TIME_IN IS NULL OR V_A_TIME_OUT IS NULL)THEN
(V_TSK_FLG IS NOT NULL AND V_TSK_Time_In IS NULL AND V_TSK_Time_Out IS Null)
(V_PRMSN_FLG IS NOT NULL AND V_PRMSN_Time_In IS NULL AND V_PRMSN_Time_Out IS Null)
IF CHK_ARTCL (P_Artcl_Clss => 4 /* Absent Id */ )
= 1 THEN
END NO_SINGNATURE_FOUND;

V_RETURN VARCHAR2(200) := NULL;
-- STRT RPRT_TYP = 1
IF RPRT_TYP = 1 THEN
IF RCRD_NO = 1 THEN V_RETURN := 6 ; -- TIME
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 2 THEN V_RETURN := 6 ; -- TIME
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 3 THEN V_RETURN := 4 ; -- HOUR
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 4 THEN V_RETURN := 4 ; -- HOUR
ELSE V_RETURN := 0;
ELSIF RPRT_TYP = 2 THEN
IF RCRD_NO = 1 THEN -- ATTENDANCE HOURS
V_RETURN := 4;
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 2 THEN -- WORKING HOURS
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 3 THEN -- NO OF MONTHS DAYS
V_RETURN := 7 ;
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 4 THEN -- NO OF RELATIVE WORKING DAYS
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 5 THEN -- NO OF DAY OFF DAYS
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 6 THEN -- NO OF EMPLOYEE WORKING DAYS THAT HE ATTENDANCE
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 7 THEN -- LAST DAY ATTENDANCE
V_RETURN := 5 ; -- DATE
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 8 THEN -- LAST DAY ABSENT
ELSIF RCRD_NO BETWEEN 9 AND 17 THEN -- FOR STATISTICES
V_RETURN := 0 ;
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 18 THEN -- SAT
V_RETURN := 7 ; -- NUMBER
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 19 THEN -- SUN
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 20 THEN -- MON
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 21 THEN -- TUE
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 22 THEN -- WED
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 23 THEN --THU
ELSIF RCRD_NO = 24 THEN -- FRI
RETURN V_RETURN ;

DAY_OFF_
FLG NUMBER ,

IF SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(F_DATE),'DAY' , 'Nls_Date_Language=AMERICAN') ,1,3) = 'WED' THEN V_RETURN := 1 ; ELSE V_RETURN := 0 ; END IF;
IF SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(F_DATE),'DAY' , 'Nls_Date_Language=AMERICAN') ,1,3) = 'THU' THEN V_RETURN := 1 ; ELSE V_RETURN := 0 ; END IF;
IF SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(F_DATE),'DAY' , 'Nls_Date_Language=AMERICAN') ,1,3) = 'FRI' THEN V_RETURN := 1 ; ELSE V_RETURN := 0 ; END IF;

FUNCTION CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY
( P_EMP_NO NUMBER ,
P_F_TIME VARCHAR2 DEFAULT '000000',
P_T_TIME VARCHAR2 DEFAULT '000000'
) RETURN NUMBER IS
SELECT NVL( COUNT (*) , 0) INTO V_COUNT FROM HRS_HLDY_DTL
WHERE HRS_HLDY_DTL.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND (P_F_DATE BETWEEN HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_DATE AND HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_DATE
OR P_T_DATE BETWEEN HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_DATE AND HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_DATE )
AND (
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_DATE, NVL(P_F_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
NVL ( HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_HLDY_DTL.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
AND NVL ( HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
OR Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_DATE, NVL(P_T_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_HLDY_DTL.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
IF V_COUNT = 0 THEN RETURN 0 ;
ELSE RETURN 1 ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN 1 ;
FUNCTION CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN
SELECT NVL( COUNT (*) , 0
) INTO V_COUNT FROM HRS_PRMSN
WHERE HRS_PRMSN.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND HRS_PRMSN.PRMSN_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_PRMSN.PRMSN_DATE, NVL(P_F_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_PRMSN.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
NVL ( HRS_PRMSN.F_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_PRMSN.PRMSN_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_PRMSN.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
AND NVL ( HRS_PRMSN.T_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_PRMSN.PRMSN_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_PRMSN.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
OR Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_PRMSN.PRMSN_DATE, NVL(P_T_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_PRMSN.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
AND HRS_PRMSN.WPRD_NO BETWEEN NVL ( P_F_WPRD_NO , HRS_PRMSN.WPRD_NO ) AND NVL( P_T_WPRD_NO , HRS_PRMSN.WPRD_NO ) ;
FUNCTION CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK
SELECT NVL( COUNT (*) , 0) INTO V_COUNT FROM HRS_TSK_MST , HRS_TSK_DTL
HRS_TSK_MST.DOC_SRL = HRS_TSK_DTL.DOC_SRL
AND HRS_TSK_DTL.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND (P_F_DATE BETWEEN HRS_TSK_MST.F_DATE AND HRS_TSK_MST.T_DATE
OR P_T_DATE BETWEEN HRS_TSK_MST.F_DATE AND HRS_TSK_MST.T_DATE )
/* AND (
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_TSK_MST.F_DATE, NVL(P_F_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_TSK_MST.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
NVL ( HRS_TSK_MST.F_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_TSK_MST.F_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_TSK_MST.F_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
AND NVL ( HRS_TSK_MST.T_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_TSK_MST.T_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_TSK_MST.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000')))
OR Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_TSK_MST.T_DATE, NVL(P_T_TIME, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_TSK_MST
.T_TIME , 'HH24MISS') , '000000') ) )
AND NVL ( HRS_TSK_MST.T_TIME , Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( HRS_TSK_MST.T_DATE, NVL ( TO_CHAR( HRS_TSK_MST.T_TIME , 'HH24MIS
S') , '000000')))
) */
AND HRS_TSK_MST.WPRD_NO BETWEEN NVL ( P_F_WPRD_NO , HRS_TSK_MST.WPRD_NO ) AND NVL( P_T_WPRD_NO , HRS_TSK_MST.WPRD_NO ) ;
--------------------------
FUNCTION GET_EMP_ATTNDNC_INFO
P_T_DEVICE_NO NUMBER
SELECT NVL( COUNT (*) , 0) INTO V_COUNT FROM HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC
WHERE EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND SGN_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE
/*AND SGN_TIME BETWEEN Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( SGN_DATE, NVL(P_F_TIME, TO_CHAR( SGN_TIME , 'HH24MISS') ) ) AND Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Chrtime_Fnc( SGN_DATE, NVL(P_T_TIME, TO_CHAR( SGN_TIME , 'HH24MISS') ) )
AND FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => SGN_DATE ) ) )
BETWEEN P_F_DAY AND P_T_DAY */
AND NVL( MCHN_NO , 0 ) BETWEEN NVL( P_F_DEVICE_NO , NVL( MCHN_NO , 0 ) ) AND NVL( P_T_DEVICE_NO , NVL( MCHN_NO , 0 ) ) ;
--AND ROWNUM = 1 ;

P_F_BRN_NO HRS_MCHN_DATA_ATTNDNC.BRN_NO%TYPE ,
RETURN FNG_TYP_LST_ABSENT_EMP_SET PIPELINED AS
OUT_REC FNG_TYP_LST_ABSENT_EMPLOYEE := FNG_TYP_LST_ABSENT_EMPLOYEE (NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL,NULL);
CURSOR GET_EMP_SHIFT ( CURSOR_F_DATE DATE , CURSOR_T_DATE DATE , CURSOR_EMP_NO NUMBER )
H.EMP_NO,
H.WPRD_NO ,
DAY_NUMBER,
Cdat,
DECODE (M.DAY_ST , 0 , 1 ,0 ) DAY_ST
To_Char(To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE ,'DD-MM-RRRR')+Level-1,'D') DAY_NUMBER,
To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE ,'DD-MM-RRRR')+Level-1 Cdat
where
FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( To_Char( To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE , 'DD-MM-RRRR') +Level-1,'D') )=
FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => To_Char( To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') +Level-1,'D'))
Connect By Level<= ( (To_Date( CURSOR_T_DATE ,'DD-MM-RRRR')) +1) - To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE ,'DD-MM-RRRR')
) , FNG_V_SHFT_GUD H , HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL M
Cdat = H.WRK_DATE
AND H.EMP_NO = CURSOR_EMP_NO
AND H.WPRD_NO BETWEEN NVL(P_F_Wprd_No , H.WPRD_NO ) AND NVL(P_T_Wprd_No , H.WPRD_NO )
AND H.WPRD_NO = M.WPRD_NO
--AND DAY_NUMBER = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => Cdat ) ) )
AND M.DAY_NO = DAY_NUMBER
AND DECODE (P_DAYOFF_EXCPT , 1 , M.DAY_ST ,0 ) = DECODE ( P_DAYOFF_EXCPT ,1 , 1 , 0 ) ;
CURSOR GET_EMP_REGULAR ( CURSOR_F_DATE DATE , CURSOR_T_DATE DATE , CURSOR_EMP_NO NUMBER )
Cdat ,
To_Char(To_Date(CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR')+Level-1,'D') DAY_NUMBER,
To_Date(CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR')+Level-1 Cdat
FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( To_Char( To_Date( CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') +Level-1,'D') )=
FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_
NO => To_Char( To_Date(CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR') +Level-1,'D'))
Connect By Level<= ( /*LAST_DAY*/ (To_Date(CURSOR_T_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR')) +1) - To_Date(CURSOR_F_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR')
) , HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP H , HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL M
Cdat BETWEEN H.F_ACTV_DATE AND H.T_ACTV_DATE
AND H.INACTIVE = 0
-- AND DAY_NUMBER = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => Cdat ) ) )
V_EMP_ATTEND_TYPE NUMBER :=0 ;
V_F_DATE DATE ;
V_T_DATE DATE ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY NUMBER := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN NUMBER := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK NUMBER := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_COUNT NUMBER := 0 ;
V_EMP_DAYOFF NUMBER := 0 ;
V_EMP_OFFICIAL NUMBER := 0 ;
IF P_F_DATE IS NULL OR P_T_DATE IS NULL AND P_PRD_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT F_DATE , T_DATE INTO V_F_DATE , V_T_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS WHERE PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO AND BRN_NO = NVL ( P_F_BRN_NO ,P_T_BRN_NO ) ;
V_F_DATE := P_F_DATE ;
V_T_DATE := P_T_DATE ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN PIPE ROW( OUT_REC );
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FOR J IN ( SELECT H.BRN_NO , H.EMP_NO , NVL(H.ATTNDNC_TYP,0) ATTNDNC_TYP --INTO V_EMP_ATTEND_TYPE
FROM S_EMP H
H.EMP_NO BETWEEN NVL( P_F_Emp_No , H.EMP_NO ) AND NVL( P_T_Emp_No , H.EMP_NO )
AND H.BRN_NO BETWEEN NVL( P_F_BRN_No , H.BRN_NO ) AND NVL( P_T_BRN_No , H.BRN_NO )
AND ( P_F_CC_CODE IS NULL OR YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val ('CC_CODE' ,H.EMP_NO) Between P_F_CC_CODE and P_T_CC_CODE )
AND ( P_F_HRCHY_NO IS NULL OR YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val ('HRCHY_NO',H.EMP_NO) Between P_F_HRCHY_NO and P_T_HRCHY_NO)
AND ( P_F_EMP_JOB_NO IS NULL OR YS_EMP_PKG.Get_Emp_Fld_val ('EMP_JOB_NO',H.EMP_NO) Between P_F_EMP_JOB_NO and P_T_EMP_JOB_NO )
IF J.ATTNDNC_TYP = 0 THEN
FOR I IN GET_EMP_REGULAR ( CURSOR_F_DATE => V_F_DATE , CURSOR_T_DATE => V_T_DATE , CURSOR_EMP_NO => J.EMP_N
O ) LOOP
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_COUNT :=
GET_EMP_ATTNDNC_INFO
( P_EMP_NO => J.EMP_NO ,
P_F_DATE => I.CDAT ,
P_T_DATE => I.CDAT ,
P_F_DAY => P_F_DAY ,
P_T_DAY => P_T_DAY ,
P_F_TIME => P_F_TIME ,
P_T_TIME => P_T_TIME ,
P_F_DEVICE_NO => P_F_DEVICE_NO ,
P_T_DEVICE_NO => P_T_DEVICE_NO
IF V_EMP_ATTNDNC_COUNT = 0
--IF P_HLDY_EXCPT = 1 THEN
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY :=
CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY ( P_EMP_NO => J.EMP_NO ,
P_T_TIME => P_T_TIME
--END IF;
--IF P_PRMSN_EXCPT = 1 THEN
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN :=
CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN
P_F_WPRD_NO => P_F_WPRD_NO ,
P_T_WPRD_NO => P_T_WPRD_NO ,
--IF P_TSK_EXCPT = 1 THEN
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK :=
CHK_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK
--IF P_DAYOFF_EXCPT = 1 THEN
V_EMP_DAYOFF := I.DAY_ST ;
-- END IF;
-- IF P_OFFICIAL_EXCPT = 1 THEN
V_EMP_OFFICIAL := FNG_GRN_PKG.IS_HLDY_DAY ( P_F_DATE =>I.CDAT , P_T_DATE => I.CDAT , P_BRN_NO => J.BRN_NO ) ;
IF (V_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY = 0 AND V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK = 0 AND V_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN = 0
AND V_EMP_OFFICIAL = 0 AND V_EMP_DAYOFF = 0 ) THEN
OUT_REC.EMP_NO := J.EMP_NO ;
OUT_REC.WPRD_NO := I.WPRD_NO;
OUT_REC.SGN_DATE := TO_CHAR (I.CDAT , 'DD-MM-RRRR') ;
OUT_REC.HLDY_FLG := V_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY ;
OUT_REC.PRMSN_FLG := V_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN ;
OUT_REC.TSK_FLG := V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK ;
OUT_REC.SIGN_COUNT := V_EMP_ATTNDNC_COUNT ;
OUT_REC.DAYOFF_FLG := V_EMP_DAYOFF;
OUT_REC.OFFICIAL_FLG := V_EMP_OFFICIAL ;
ELSIF J.ATTNDNC_TYP = 1 THEN
FOR I IN GET_EMP_SHIFT ( CURSOR_F_DATE => V_F_DATE , CURSOR_T_DATE => V_T_DATE , CURSOR_EMP_NO => J.EMP_NO ) LOOP
OUT_REC.
TSK_FLG := V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_HLDY := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_PRMSN := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_TSK := 0 ;
V_EMP_ATTNDNC_COUNT := 0 ;
V_EMP_DAYOFF := 0 ;
V_EMP_OFFICIAL := 0 ;
END LOOP ;

END Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg;

CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY FNG_GRN_PKG AS
******************************************************************************/
FUNCTION GET_PRD_NO (P_DATE IN DATE , P_YEAR OUT NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER IS
Select S_PRD_DTL.PRD_NO , S_PRD_DTL.YR_NO INTO V_PRD_NO , P_YEAR
From S_PRD_DTL
Where P_DATE Between F_DATE and T_DATE ;
RETURN V_PRD_NO ;
P_YEAR := To_Number (To_Char (P_DATE, 'YYYY')) ;
Return (To_Number (To_Char (P_DATE, 'MM')));
Function Get_Wprd_Typ ( P_Wprd_No In HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.Wprd_No%Type) Return Number IS
v_wprd_typ HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.Wprd_Typ%Type;
Select Wprd_Typ
Into v_wprd_typ
From HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST
Where Wprd_No = P_Wprd_No;
Return(v_wprd_typ);
v_wprd_typ:=0;
END Get_Wprd_Typ;
/*_________________________________________________________
_____________________________________________________*/
FUNCTION Get_Wprd_Typ_Nm( P_Wprd_No In HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.Wprd_No%Type ) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
v_wprd_typ_Nm IAS_LABELS.CAPTION_DET%Type;
v_wprd_typ := Get_Wprd_Typ ( P_Wprd_No =>P_Wprd_No) ;
RETURN IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Flg_Nm (P_flg_code=>'WPRD_TYP' , P_flg_value => v_wprd_typ ,P_Lng_no => IAS_PRMTR_PKG.GetPval(p_prmtr =>'LANG_NO') ) ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL ;
END Get_Wprd_Typ_Nm;
PROCEDURE Get_Wprd_Typ_Nm( P_Wprd_No In HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.Wprd_No%Type , P_PLCY_NO OUT HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.WPRD_TYP%TYPE ,P_PLCY_NM OUT VARCHAR2 ) IS
P_PLCY_NM := IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Flg_Nm (P_flg_code=>'WPRD_TYP' , P_flg_value => v_wprd_typ ,P_Lng_no => IAS_PRMTR_PKG.GetPval(p_prmtr =>'LANG_NO') ) ;
P_PLCY_NO := v_wprd_typ ;
FUNCTION Get_SHFT_GRP_NM (P_GRP_NO In FNG_SHFT_GRP.GRP_NO%TYPE, P_LNG_NO In NUMBER ) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
V_WGRPnm FNG_SHFT_GRP.GRP_L_NM%TYPE;
IF P_GRP_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
Select Decode (P_LNG_NO,1,GRP_L_NM,NVL(GRP_F_NM,GRP_L_NM))
Into V_WGRPnm
From FNG_SHFT_GRP
Where GRP_NO = P_GRP_NO
and RowNum<=1;
Return(V_WGRPnm);
Return(Null);
End Get_SHFT_GRP_NM;
FUNCTION Get_EMP_NM (P_EMP_NO In S_EMP.EMP_NO%TYPE, P_LNG_NO In NUMBER ) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
V_EMPnm S_EMP.EMP_L_NM %TYPE;
IF P_EMP_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
Select Decode (P_LNG_NO,1,EMP_L_NM,NVL(EMP_F_NM,EMP_L_NM))
Into V_EMPnm
From S_EMP
Where EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
Return(V_EMPnm);
End Get_EMP_NM;
FUNCTION IS_HLDY_DAY ( P_F_DATE IN HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY.PRD_DATE%TYPE , P_T_DATE IN HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY.PRD_DATE%TYPE , P_BRN_NO NUMBER DEFAULT NULL ) RETURN NUMBER
V_HLDY_DAYS_NO NUMBER ;
IF P_F_DATE IS NOT NULL OR P_T_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT COUNT(PRD_DATE) INTO V_HLDY_DAYS_NO FROM HRS_PRD_DAY M
WHERE PRD_DATE BETWEEN TO_DATE( NVL(P_F_DATE , P_T_DATE),'DD-MM-RRRR') AND TO_DATE(NVL(P_T_DATE, P_F_DATE) ,'DD-MM-RRRR')
AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY D
WHERE PRD_DATE = M.PRD_DATE
AND ( NVL(D.BRN_NO,0) = DECODE(P_BRN_NO,NULL,NVL(D.BRN_NO,0),P_BRN_NO) OR BRN_NO Is Null)
AND NVL(D.INACTIVE ,0) = 0
AND ROWNUM<=1);
RETURN V_HLDY_DAYS_NO ;
SELECT (TO_DATE(P_T_DATE,'DD/MM/YYYY') - TO_DATE(P_F_DATE,'DD/MM/YYYY'))+1 INTO V_HLDY_DAYS_NO FROM DUAL;
RETURN (V_HLDY_DAYS_NO);
RETURN(0);
Return 0 ;
END IS_HLDY_DAY;

PROCEDURE SET_ENVIROMENT_PARAMETERS IS
V_STRT_WEEK_DAY_NO NUMBER ;
SELECT STRT_WEEK_DAY_NO INTO V_DAY_NO FROM FNG_PARA ;
IF V_DAY_NO IS NULL THEN
-- GETTING DATA BASE NLS_TERRITORY
SELECT T.VALUE INTO V_NLS_TERRITORY FROM NLS_SESSION_PARAMETERS T WHERE T.PARAMETER = 'NLS_TERRITORY';
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_NLS_TERRITORY := 'YEMEN' ;
-- GETTING DAY NO RELATED TO DATA BASE NLS_TERRITORY
SELECT FNG_NLS_TERRITORY.STRT_WEEK_DAY INTO V_DAY_NO FROM FNG_NLS_TERRITORY WHERE COUNTRY = V_NLS_TERRITORY ;
WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
INSRT_FNG_NLS_TERRITORY ;
WHEN OTHERS THEN V_DAY_NO := 1 ;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_DAY_NO := 1 ;
END SET_ENVIROMENT_PARAMETERS;
FUNCTION GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY (P_DAY_NO NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER AS
V_CALC NUMBER ;
SET_ENVIROMENT_PARAMETERS;
V_CALC := MOD ( (7 - V_DAY_NO)+1 + P_DAY_NO , 7 ) ;
IF V_CALC = 0 THEN RETURN 7 ; ELSE RETURN V_CALC ; END IF;
END GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ;
FUNCTION GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY NUMBER) RETURN NUMBER AS
V_CALC NUMBER;
V_CALC := MOD ( ( V_DAY_NO - 1 ) + P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY , 7) ;
END GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ;
FUNCTION Get_DAY_NM (P_flg_code In varchar2,P_flg_value In Number ,P_Lng_no In Number Default 1) RETURN VARCHAR2 Is
v_flg_nm varchar2(100);
Select flg_desc
Into v_flg_nm
From s_flags
Where lang_no = P_Lng_no
and flg_code = P_flg_code
and flg_value= FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO (P_flg_value )
Return(v_flg_nm);
When Others Then
End Get_DAY_NM;
FUNCTION Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE In DATE ) RETURN NUMBER Is
RETURN TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(P_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR'),'D' );
END Get_DAY_NO;
FUNCTION Get_DAY_NO (P_DAY_NM In VARCHAR2 ) RETURN NUMBER Is
IF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'SAT' THEN RETURN 1;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'SUN' THEN RETURN 2;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'MON' THEN RETURN 3;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'TUE' THE
N RETURN 4;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'WED' THEN RETURN 5;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM) = 'THU' THEN RETURN 6;
ELSIF UPPER(P_DAY_NM
) = 'FRI' THEN RETURN 7;
PROCEDURE GET_PRD_INTERVAL (P_WPRD_NO HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL.WPRD_NO%TYPE, P_F_DATE DATE , P_T_DATE DATE ,P_RET_F_TIME OUT VARCHAR2 , P_RET_T_TIME OUT VARCHAR2 ) IS
IF P_F_DATE IS NOT NULL AND P_T_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN
TO_CHAR ( MIN(TIME_IN) , 'HH24:MI') ,
TO_CHAR ( MAX(TIME_OUT) , 'HH24:MI')
INTO P_RET_F_TIME , P_RET_T_TIME
WHERE WPRD_NO = P_WPRD_NO
AND DAY_NO = DECODE (P_F_DATE ,P_T_DATE , FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => P_F_DATE ) ) ) ,DAY_NO) ;
P_RET_F_TIME := NULL;
P_RET_T_TIME := NULL;
WHERE WPRD_NO = P_WPRD_NO ;
Function Get_PLCY_NM ( P_PLCY_No In FNG_PLCY_CODE_MST.PLCY_NO%Type, P_LNG_NO In NUMBER ) Return VARCHAR2 IS
V_PLCY_NM VARCHAR2 (2000);
IF P_PLCY_No IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT DECODE(P_LNG_NO,1,M.PLCY_L_NM,M.PLCY_F_NM) INTO V_PLCY_NM
FROM FNG_PLCY_CODE_MST M
M.PLCY_NO = P_PLCY_No ;
RETURN V_PLCY_NM ;
END Get_PLCY_NM;
V_Doc_Srl_Typ Number := P_Doc_Srl_Typ;
If P_Doc_Typ Is Not Null And V_Doc_Srl_Typ Is Null Then
V_Doc_Srl_Typ:= HRS_DOC_SQ.Get_Doc_Typ_Serial (P_DOC_TYP => P_Doc_Typ );
If V_Doc_Srl_
Typ Is Null Then
Raise_Application_Error (-20001,'Error When Select Serial Typ For Document , '||Sqlerrm);
If P_Doc_Typ = 732 Then -- REGULAR SHFTING
-- Serial By Branch + Doc_Jv_Typ + -- Accumlated By Branch
Select nvl(Max(doc_no),0) + 1 Into V_Doc_No From FNG_RGLR_SHFT_MST
where Brn_No = P_Brn_No;
ELSIf P_Doc_Typ = 733 Then -- VARIABLE SHFTING
Select nvl(Max(doc_no),0) + 1 Into V_Doc_No From FNG_VRBL_SHFT_MST
Return(V_Doc_No);
Return(null);
END Get_Doc_No;
FUNCTION CHK_DATE_OVERLAPPING ( P_F_CMP_DATE IN DATE , P_T_CMP_DATE IN DATE , P_F_DATE IN DATE , P_T_DATE IN DATE ) RETURN NUMBER IS
V_RAISED NUMBER := 0 ;
/* CHECKING DATE OVERLAPPING */
IF TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'MM') = TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'MM') THEN
IF TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'DD') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'DD') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'DD') OR
TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'DD') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'DD') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'DD') OR
TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'DD') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'DD') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'DD') OR
TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'DD') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'DD') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'DD')
V_RAISED := 1 ;
ELSIF TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'MM') <> TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'MM') THEN
IF TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'MM') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'MM') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'MM') OR
TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'MM') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'MM') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'MM') OR
TO_CHAR(P_F_DATE,'MM') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'MM') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'MM') OR
TO_CHAR(P_T_DATE,'MM') BETWEEN TO_CHAR(P_F_CMP_DATE,'MM') AND TO_CHAR(P_T_CMP_DATE,'MM')
RETURN V_RAISED ;
END CHK_DATE_OVERLAPPING;
Function Get_Flw_Date_Fnc(P_Date Date ) Return Date Is
V_Date Date;
Select To_Date(P_Date,'DD/MM/RRRR') + 1 Into V_Date From Dual;
Return V_Date;
End Get_Flw_Date_Fnc;

V_Fnd:=0;
End Chk_Plcy_Fnc;

Mst.Plcy_No = P_Plcy_No

V_Day_Hours HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL.WPRD_HOUR%Type;

V_DAY_OFF_FLG NUMBER(1) ;

Elsif P_Day_Flw_Flg = 0 Then

Else --// Open Work Period

V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc := IS_HLDY_DAY ( P_F_DATE => P_Fdate , P_T_DATE => P_Fdate , P_BRN_NO => P_Brn_No ) ;
IF (P_Wprd_Typ = 2 AND V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc = 1 AND P_Assgn_Flg = 1 )
OR (P_Wprd_Typ = 2 AND P_Assgn_Flg = 1 ) THEN -- ASGNMENT PERIOD

V_DAY_OFF_FLG := 0 ;
(P_Wprd_Typ IN ( 3 , 4) AND V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc = 1 And V_Fng_Plcy_Cod_Mst.OFF_CALC_WRK_MNDRTY =0 ) then
V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc := 1 ;
(P_Wprd_Typ IN ( 3 , 4) AND V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc = 1 And V_Fng_Plcy_Cod_Mst.OFF_CALC_WRK_MNDRTY =1 AND P_DAY_OFF = 0 )
(P_Wprd_Typ IN ( 3 , 4) AND P_DAY_OFF = 0 ) THEN -- SHIFTING AND OVERTIME PERIOD
(P_Wprd_Typ = 1 AND V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc = 1 And V_Fng_Plcy_Cod_Mst.OFF_CALC_WRK_MNDRTY = 0 AND P_DAY_OFF = 0 )
V_DAY_OFF_FLG := 1 ;
(P_Wprd_Typ = 1 AND V_Is_Ofcl_Day_Fnc = 1 And V_Fng_Plcy_Cod_Mst.OFF_CALC_WRK_MNDRTY =1 AND P_DAY_OFF = 0 ) THEN
ELSIF P_DAY_OFF = 1 THEN -- DAYS OFF
ELSIF P_DAY_OFF IS NULL OR P_DAY_OFF = 0 THEN -- DAYS OFF

Procedure Insrt_Gud_Assgn_Prc( P_Brn_No IN S_BRN.BRN_NO%Type,
P_Trmnl In Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.AD_Trmnl_N
m%Type,
V_Erorr Number(1);
V_Prl_Prd_Dtl := Get_Vald_Prd_Rw_Fnc( P_Yr_No => P_Yr_No,
P_BRN_No => P_Brn_No,
P_F_Date => P_Date,
P_T_Date => P_Date,
P_Erorr => V_Erorr );
If V_Erorr =0 Then
Insrt_Gud_Tbl_Prc( P_Brn_No => P_Brn_No,
P_Plcy_No => P_Plcy_No,
P_Yr_No => P_Yr_No,
P_Emp_no => P_Emp_No,
P_Trmnl => P_Trmnl,
P_Crt_Usr => P_Crt_Usr,
P_Hrchy_No => P_Hrchy_No,
P_Wprd_No => P_Wprd_No,
P_Wprd_Typ => P_Wprd_Typ,
P_F_Time_In => P_F_Time_In,
P_Time_In => P_Time_In,
P_T_Time_In => P_T_Time_In,
P_Day_Flw_Flg => P_Day_Flw_Flg,
P_F_Time_Out => P_F_Time_Out,
P_Time_Out => P_Time_Out,
P_T_Time_Out => P_T_Time_Out,
P_Day_Hours => P_Day_Hours,
P_Fdate => P_Date ,
P_Tdate => P_Date ,
P_Assgn_Flg => P_Assgn_Flg,
P_INCRS_MNT_TIME => P_INCRS_MNT_TIME,
P_RST_FLG =>P_RST_FLG
End Insrt_Gud_Assgn_Prc;
Where INACTIVE = 0 And ATTNDNC_APPRVD_FLG = 0
AND BRN_No = P_BRN_No
End Get_Vald_Prd_Rw_Fnc;
P_Shft_Grp_No In FNG_SHFT_GRP.GRP_NO%Type Default Null,
P_RST_FLG IN HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.RST_FLG %Type,
P_DAY_OFF IN NUMBER DEFAULT 0
V_Day_Hours Number;
DAY_NO,
Cdat

prdt,'DD-MM-RRRR')+Level-1 Cdat

Connect By Level<= ( /*LAST_DAY*/ (To_Date(P_Eprdt,'DD-MM-RRRR')) +1) - To_Date(P_Sprdt,'DD-MM-RRRR')
) , HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP H
AND H.EMP_NO = P_Emp_No AND WPRD_NO = P_Wprd_No
GET_EMP_CLC_BEFORE (

) = 0 THEN

--##------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
Procedure ASSIGNMENT_Prc
P_HRCHY_NO IN Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.HRCHY_NO%TYPE ,
P_WPRD_NO IN Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.WPRD_NO%TYPE ,
FOR I IN (
Select D.Emp_No ,
M.Wprd_No ,
A.WPRD_TYP ,
M.PLCY_NO ,
K.PRD_DATE F_DATE ,
DECODE ( S.TIME_HAS_FLW ,1 , K.PRD_DATE + 1 ,K.PRD_DATE ) T_DATE ,
S.TIME_HAS_FLW ,
M.DUR_TIME ,
S.INCRS_MNT_TIME ,
E.HRCHY_NO ,
M.BRN_NO ,
M.BRN_USR ,
M.BRN_YEAR ,
M.F_Time TIME_IN ,
M.T_Time TIME_OUT ,
M.F_Time_In ,
M.T_Time_In ,
M.F_Time_Out ,
M.T_Time_Out ,
K.PRD_NO ,
M.DOC_SRL ,
M.DOC_NO
From S_EMP E,
HRS_ADD_ASGN_MST M,
HRS_ADD_ASGN_DTL D,
HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL R ,
HRS_PRD_DAY K, /* LOOP THROUGHT ASSIGNMENT DATES FROM DATE - TO -DATE */
HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST A,
HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL S
WHERE E.EMP_NO = D.EMP_NO
AND K.PRD_DATE BETWEEN TO_DATE( NVL(M.F_DATE , M.T_DATE ),'DD-MM-RRRR') AND TO_DATE(NVL(M.T_DATE , M.F_DATE ) ,'DD-MM-RRRR')
AND M.Doc_Srl = D.Doc_Srl
And M.BRN_No = P_Brn_No
And D.Emp_No = P_Emp_No
And K.PRD_DATE Between P_F_Date And P_T_Date
And R.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => K.PRD_DATE ) ) )-- 04/07/2015
AND R.WPRD_NO = M.WPRD_NO
AND M.APPROVED = 1 -- CHANGED FROM 2 TO 1 ... CAUSE CHANGED APPROVED LIST VALUES
AND A.Wprd_No = S.Wprd_No
And A.Wprd_No = M.Wprd_No
And S.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( P_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY => FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( P_DAY_NO => FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO (P_DATE => K.PRD_DATE ) ) )
AND S.DAY_ST = 1
Insrt_Gud_Tbl_Prc(
P_Brn_No => I.BRN_NO ,
P_Brn_Usr => I.BRN_USR ,
P_Plcy_No => I.PLCY_NO ,
P_Yr_No => I.BRN_YEAR ,
P_Hrchy_No => I.HRCHY_NO ,
p_Wprd_Typ => I.WPRD_TYP ,
P_F_Time_In => I.F_Time_IN ,
P_Time_In => I.Time_In,
P_T_Time_In => I.T_Time_IN,
P_Day_Flw_Flg => I.TIME_HAS_FLW ,
P_F_Time_Out => I.F_Time_Out ,
P_Time_Out => I.Time_Out ,
P_T_Time_Out => I.T_Time_Out ,
P_Day_Hours => I.DUR_TIME ,
P_Fdate => I.F_DATE ,
P_Tdate => I.T_DATE ,
P_Assgn_Flg => 1 ,
P_Doc_Typ => NULL,
P_Shft_Grp_No => NULL,
P_INCRS_MNT_TIME => I.INCRS_MNT_TIME,
P_RST_FLG => 0,
P_DAY_OFF => 0,
INSERT INTO Fng_Stndr_Clc_DTL (STNDR_SRL,TRANS_TYP,TRANS_NO,DUR,TIME_IN,TIME_OUT ,
AD_U_ID , AD_DATE ,CMP_NO ,BRN_NO ,BRN_YEAR ,BRN_USR ,DOC_SRL
VALUES
(V_SR ,4/*ASSIGNMENT*/ ,I.DOC_No ,I.DUR_TIME,TO_CHAR(I.Time_IN,'HH24:MI:SS'), TO_CHAR(I.Time_OUT,
'HH24:MI:SS') ,

, SYSDATE , IAS_Brn_Pkg.Get_Br_Cmp ( G_BRN_NO => P_Brn_No) ,P_Brn_No ,P_BRN_YEAR , P_Brn_Usr
, I.DOC_SRL
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'ASSIGNMENT_Prc >> '||SQLERRM );
V_PRD_NO := P_PRD_NO ;

FUNCTION GET_HLDY_DSC ( P_HLDY_DATE DATE ) RETURN VARCHAR2
V_HLDY_DSC VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY.HLDY_DSC INTO V_HLDY_DSC
FROM HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY
HRS_PRD_DAY_HLDY.PRD_DATE = P_HLDY_DATE
RETURN V_HLDY_DSC;
FUNCTION GET_GRP_BY_FLD_FNC(P_GRP_BY NUMBER) RETURN VARCHAR2 IS
V_GRP_FLD VARCHAR2(500):= NULL;
IF P_GRP_BY = 1 THEN
V_GRP_FLD := 'M.WPRD_NO';
ELSIF P_GRP_BY = 2 THEN
V_GRP_FLD := 'M.EMP_NO';
ELSIF P_GRP_BY = 3 THEN
V_GRP_FLD := 'M.HRCHY_NO';
ELSIF P_GRP_BY = 4 THEN
V_GRP_FLD := 'DECODE (M.EMP_NO,NULL,NULL, IAS_Gen_Pkg.Get_Fld_Value (''S_EMP'' ,''EMP_JOB_NO'' ,''WHERE EMP_NO=''||M.EMP_NO)) JOB_NO ' ;
ELSIF P_GRP_BY = 5 THEN
V_GRP_FLD := 'M.F_DATE';
V_GRP_FLD := 'M.BRN_NO';
RETURN V_GRP_FLD;
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN 'M.BRN_NO';
FUNCTION GET_JOB_NO (P_EMP_NO NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER
P_JOB_NO NUMBER ;
SELECT DECODE (P_EMP_NO,NULL,NULL, IAS_Gen_Pkg
.Get_Fld_Value ('S_EMP' ,'EMP_JOB_NO' ,'WHERE EMP_NO='||P_EMP_NO))
P_JOB_NO
RETURN P_JOB_NO ;
EXCEPTI
ON WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN NULL;

(P_F_Emp_No,NULL ,Emp_No,P_F_Emp_No) AND DECODE(P_T_Emp_No,NULL ,Emp_No,P_T_Emp_No)

END DLT_STNDR_ACTL_DATA;

PROCEDURE DEBUGING ( P_TEXT VARCHAR2 ) IS
--INSERT INTO DEBUG VALUES ( P_TEXT ) ; COMMIT;
PROCEDURE DEBUGING_ACT ( P_TEXT VARCHAR2 , P_Value_num Number Default Null ) IS
--INSERT INTO DEBUG_ACT VALUES ( P_TEXT /*, P_Value_num */) ; COMMIT ;
URN FNG_TYP_ATTENDENCE_TIME_SET PIPELINED
OUT_REC FNG_TYP_ATTENDENCE_TIME := FNG_TYP_ATTENDENCE_TIME (NULL,NULL) ;
V_DATE DATE;
V_cnt NUMBER;
V_ACTV_DATE_CNT NUMBER:=0;
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG NUMBER;
V_attending_typ Number;
V_CUT_OFFICIAL_DAY_FRM_HLDY Number;
V_Cut_
Day_Off_Frm_Hldy Number;
CURSOR ALL_DAYS(P_F_DATE DATE ,P_T_DATE DATE) IS SELECT PRD_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_DAY M
WHERE PRD_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE ;
/*CURSOR SHIFTING IS SELECT M.PRD_DATE , E.DAY_OFF FROM HRS_PRD_DAY M
PRD_DATE BETWEEN P_HLDY_F_DATE AND P_HLDY_T_DATE
And FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO ( PRD_DATE ) ) )
NOT IN ( SELECT H.DAY_NO FROM HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL H , FNG_V_SHFT_GUD E
E.WPRD_NO = H.WPRD_NO
AND E.EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
ND H.DAY_ST = 1
AND E.DAY_OFF = 0
) ORDER BY PRD_DATE ASC ;*/
CURSOR SHIFTING IS
SELECT M.PRD_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_DAY M
WHERE M.PRD_DATE BETWEEN P_HLDY_F_DATE AND P_HLDY_T_DATE
AND M.PRD_DATE NOT IN (
SELECT E.WRK_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL H , FNG_V_SHFT_GUD E
and WRK_DATE between P_HLDY_F_DATE AND P_HLDY_T_DATE
And FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO ( E.WRK_DATE ) ) ) = H.DAY_NO
) ORDER BY PRD_DATE ASC ;
-- GETTING REGULARE ATTENDANCE TYPE ....OR ..... SHIFTING ATTENDANCE TYPE .
Select Nvl(ATTNDNC_TYP,0) Into V_attending_typ from s_emp where emp_no=P_Emp_No;
Select nvl(Cut_Official_Day_Frm_Hldy,0),
nvl(Cut_Day_Off_Frm_Hldy,0)
Into V_
CUT_OFFICIAL_DAY_FRM_HLDY,
V_Cut_Day_Off_Frm_Hldy
From Hrs_Hldy_Code M
Where Nvl(Inactive,0) = 0
And M.Hldy_No = P_Hldy_No
And Rownum=1 ;
IF V_attending_typ = 0 then -- REGULARE ATTENDANCE TYPE ....
FOR I IN ALL_DAYS(P_F_DATE=> P_HLDY_F_DATE ,P_T_DATE=> P_HLDY_T_DATE ) LOOP
V_Cnt := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt ('Select NVL( COUNT(*) , 0 ) From HRS_V_PRD_DAY_HLDY Where PRD_DATE ='''||I.PRD_DATE||''' and BRN_NO ='||P_BRN_NO ||' and RowNum<=1');
-- CHECKING IS DATE OFFICIAL DAY ...
If Nvl(V_cnt,0)> 0 Then
IF V_CUT_OFFICIAL_DAY_FRM_HLDY=1 THEN
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG:=0; -- RETURN 0 MEAN IS OFFICIAL DAY ...... SO NOT RETURN
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG:=1; -- RETURN 1 MEAN IS NOT OFFICIAL DAY ... SO THE DATE IS RETURN
-- CHECKING IS DATE DAY OFF ...
IF V_INACTV_DAY_FLG=1 Then
IF IS_WRK_PRD_DATE (P_PRD_DATE => I.PRD_DATE , P_Emp_no=> P_EMP_NO ) = 1 THEN --- Day-off days
IF V_CUT_DAY_OFF_FRM_HLDY=1 THEN
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG:=0;
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG:=1;
-------------------------------------------
IF V_INACTV_DAY_FLG=1 THEN
OUT_REC.SGN_DATE := I.PRD_DATE ;
------------
V_INACTV_DAY_FLG := 0 ;
FOR Shft IN SHIFTING LOOP
V_Cnt := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Cnt ('Select NVL( COUNT(*) , 0 ) From HRS_V_PRD_DAY_HLDY Where PRD_DATE ='''||SHFT.PRD_DATE||''' and BRN_NO ='||P_BRN_NO ||' and RowNum<=1');
IF V_CUT_DAY_OFF_FRM_HLDY=1 THEN -- Day-off days
OUT_REC.SGN_DATE := Shft.PRD_DATE ;
--##-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------##--
FUNCTION IS_WRK_PRD_DATE (P_PRD_DATE DATE , P_Emp_no NUMBER ) RETURN NUMBER
v_prd_date date;
V_ERROR VARCHAR2(200);
SELECT PRD_DATE INTO V_PRD_DATE FROM hrs_v_prd_day
WHERE prd_date = P_PRD_DATE
AND fng_grn_pkg.get_coressponding_day_no (
fng_grn_pkg.get_day_no_rltd_nls_territory (TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(P_PRD_DATE,'DD-MM-RRRR'),'D' )
)) NOT IN ( SELECT h.day_no
FROM hrs_prd_wrk_dfn_dtl h, hrs_prd_wrk_emp e
WHERE e.wprd_no = h.wprd_no
AND e.emp_no = P_Emp_no
AND h.day_st = 1
AND NVL (e.inactive, 0) = 0
and P_PRD_DATE between e.F_ACTV_DATE and E.t_ACTV_DATE );
IF V_PRD_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN
return 1;
RETURN 0;
WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN Null;

Function Get_Last_Period_Day ( P_BRN_NO HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS.BRN_NO%TYPE , P_PRD_NO HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS.PRD_NO%TYPE , P_EMP_NO S_EMP.EMP_NO%TYPE ) RETURN DATE
V_END_PERIOD_DATE DATE ;
V_EMP_END_PERIOD_DATE DATE ;
V_EMP_Attending_Type NUMBER := 0 ;
-- GETTING END PERIOD DATE FROM TABLE HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS
SELECT T_FNG_DATE INTO V_END_PERIOD_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS
AND PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO ;
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
----
Select Nvl(ATTNDNC_TYP,0) Into V_EMP_Attending_Type from S_
emp where emp_no = P_Emp_No;
--__________________________________
-- REGULAR ATENDANCE TYPE ....
IF V_EMP_Attending_Type = 0 THEN
MAX (D.T_ACTV_DATE) INTO V_EMP_END_PERIOD_DATE
From S_Emp S,HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP D ,HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS P
S.Emp_NO = D.Emp_NO
And S.Brn_No = P_BRN_NO
And S.Emp_St = 1
AND D.INACTIVE = 0
AND P.BRN_NO = S.BRN_NO
AND P.PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO
S.EMP_NO_MCHN
IS NOT NULL
AND ( ( P.F_FNG_DATE BETWEEN D.F_ACTV_DATE AND D.T_ACTV_DATE OR P.T_FNG_DATE BETWEEN D.F_ACTV_DATE AND D.T_ACTV_DATE )
( D.F_ACTV_DATE BETWEEN P.F_FNG_DATE AND P.T_FNG_DATE OR D.T_ACTV_DATE BETWEEN P.F_FNG_DATE AND P.T_FNG_DATE )
-- SHIFTING ATTENDANCE TYPE .....
ELSIF V_EMP_Attending_Type <> 0 THEN
SELECT MAX( S.WRK_DATE) INTO V_EMP_END_PERIOD_DATE
AND P.BRN_NO = P_BRN_NO
AND E.BRN_NO = P.BRN_NO
AND P.PRD_NO = TO_CHAR(WRK_DATE,'MM')
AND S.WRK_DATE BETWEEN P.F_FNG_DATE AND P.T_FNG_DATE
AND E.EMP_NO_MCHN IS NOT NULL ;
-- COMPARING LAST DATE FOR CALCULATED PERIOD OR LAST DATE FOR EMP LINK FOR WORKING ...
IF V_EMP_END_PERIOD_DATE > V_END_PERIOD_DATE THEN
RETURN V_END_PERIOD_DATE ;
RETURN V_EMP_END_PERIOD_DATE ;
Procedure Get_Period_Date_Range ( P_BRN_NO HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS.BRN_NO%TYPE , P_PRD_NO HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS.PRD_NO%TYPE , R_STRT_PERIOD_DATE OUT DATE , R_END_PERIOD_DATE OUT DATE )
V_Strt_PERIOD_DATE DATE ;
SELECT F_FNG_DATE , T_FNG_DATE INTO V_Strt_PERIOD_DATE, V_END_PERIOD_DATE FROM HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS
R_STRT_PERIOD_DATE := V_Strt_PERIOD_DATE;
R_END_PERIOD_DATE := V_End_PERIOD_DATE ;
--##-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------
V_Attending_Type NUMBER ;
V_COUNT_WPRD_NO NUMBER := 0;
V_ALL_WPRD_HOUR NUMBER := 0;
V_FACTOR NUMBER := 0 ;
V_ACCUMLTV_WRK_VALUE NUMBER := 0;
V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE NUMBER := 0;
V_NET_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_OFFCIAL_Cnt NUMBER := 0;
V_Cnt NUMBER := 0;
V_EMP_CNT NUMBER := 0;
V_BRN_NO NUMBER := P_BRN_NO_;
V_OFFCAL_HOUR_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_OFFCAL_MINUTE_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_OFFCAL_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_DAYS_MINUTE_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_DAYS_VAL NUMBER := 0;
V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE NUMBER := 0;
IF P_PRD_NO IS NULL THEN
-- ASSIGN DEFAULT F_DATE & T_DATE
IF P_F_DATE IS NULL THEN
V_F_DATE := Hrs_Gnr_Pkg.Get_Frst_Day;
V_F_DATE
:= P_F_DATE ;
IF P_T_DATE IS NULL THEN
V_T_DATE := Ys_Gen_Pkg.Get_Sysdate ;
ELSIF P_PRD_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
SELECT NVL(F_FNG_DATE , F_DATE) , NVL(T_FNG_DATE, T_DATE ) INTO V_F_DATE , V_T_DATE
FROM HRS_PRD_BRN_CLS
WHERE BRN_NO = NVL( P_BRN_NO_ /*V_BRN_NO */, BRN_NO )
AND ROWNUM = 1 ; -- JUST ONE ROW RETURN
-------------------------------------
--## First lOOP Throught Branch Employee
FOR BRN IN ( SELECT BRN_NO FROM S_BRN
BRN_NO = NVL(P_BRN_NO_ , BRN_NO )
ORDER BY BRN_NO ASC
-----------------------------------------------
V_BRN_NO := BRN.BRN_NO ;
--## First lOOP Throught Employee
FOR EMP IN ( SELECT EMP_NO FROM S_EMP
BRN_NO = V_BRN_NO
AND EMP_NO BETWEEN NVL(P_F_EMP_NO , EMP_NO ) AND NVL(P_T_EMP_NO , EMP_NO )
AND NVL(HRCHY_NO,0) = NVL(P_HRCHY_NO , NVL(HRCHY_NO,0) )
AND NVL(CC_CODE,0) = NVL(P_CC_CODE , NVL(CC_CODE,0 ) )
AND EMP_ST = 1
ORDER BY BRN_NO , EMP_NO , HRCHY_NO
V_EMP_CNT := V_EMP_CNT + 1 ;
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
------
Select Nvl(ATTNDNC_TYP,0) Into V_attending_type from s_emp where Emp_no = Emp.Emp_No;
IF 1=1 THEN --V_Attending_Type = 0 then -- REGULARE ATTENDANCE TYPE ....
-------------------------------------------------------------
FOR WORKING_DAYS IN
-- LOOP THROUGH WORKING DAYES FORM EMPLOYEE .....
SELECT M.DAT WRK_DATE , E.WPRD_NO , H.WPRD_HOUR , H.DAY_ST
TABLE (Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.GET_DAY_OFF_DATES2 ( P_Sprdt => V_F_DATE ,P_Eprdt => V_T_DATE )) M, -- LOOP THROUGHT ASSIGNMENT DATES FROM DATE - TO -DATE
HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_DTL H,
Hrs_prd_wrk_emp E
V_Attending_Type = 0
AND H.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO ( M.DAT ) ) )
AND E.WPRD_NO = H.WPRD_NO
AND E.WPRD_NO = NVL(P_WPRD_NO , E.WPRD_NO )
AND E.EMP_NO = EMP.EMP_NO -- EMPLOYEE IN E_EMP CURSOR ...
AND 0 = NVL (E.inactive, 0) -- LINKED PERIOD FOR EMPLOYEE IS ACTIVE
AND M.DAT Between E.F_ACTV_DATE AND E.T_ACTV_DATE
AND M.DAT Between V_F_DATE AND V_T_DATE

SELECT E.WRK_DATE WRK_DATE , E.WPRD_NO , H.WPRD_HOUR , H.DAY_ST
FNG_V_SHFT_GUD E

AND H.DAY_NO = FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_CORESSPONDING_DAY_NO ( FNG_GRN_PKG.GET_DAY_NO_RLTD_NLS_TERRITORY ( FNG_GRN_PKG.Get_DAY_NO ( E.WRK_DATE ) ) )
AND E.WRK_DATE Between E.F_DATE AND E.T_DATE

IF V_Attending_Type = 0 THEN
FROM HRS_PRD
_WRK_DFN_DTL H , HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST S

FROM FNG_V_SHFT_GUD S

V_ALL_WPRD_HOUR := 0 ;
V_COUNT_WPRD_NO := 0 ;
V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE := Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Transfer_From_Unit_To_Unit2
p_unit_val => V_ALL_WPRD_HOUR ,
V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL :=
Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.Transfer_From_Unit_To_Unit2
p_unit_val => WORKING_DAYS.WPRD_HOUR ,
IF WORKING_DAYS.DAY_ST = 1 THEN
--------------------------------
IF P_TYPE = 1 THEN -- HOUR
V_ACCUMLTV_WRK_VALUE :=V_ACCUMLTV_WRK_VALUE + V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL ;
ELSIF P_TYPE = 2 THEN -- DAY
IF P_WPRD_MTHD = 1 THEN -- CLC PER PERIOD FIXED PERCENT
ELSIF P_WPRD_MTHD = 2 THEN -- CLC PER PERIOD HOURS PERCENT
V_FACTOR := ( V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL / V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE ) ;
V_ACCUMLTV_WRK_VALUE := V_ACCUMLTV_WRK_VALUE + V_FACTOR ;
ELSIF WORKING_DAYS.DAY_ST = 0 THEN
----------------------------------
V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE := V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE + V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL ;
V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE := V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE + V_FACTOR ;
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--## Determine Equation ...
---------------------------
--## 1. GET COUNT DAYS BETWEEN SENDING DATE S FROM & TO
--SELECT NVL( COUNT(*) , 0 )INTO V_Cnt FROM TABLE (Fng_Actl_Clc_Pkg.GET_DAY_OFF_DATES2 ( P_Sprdt => P_F_DATE,P_Eprdt => P_T_DATE )) ;
V_DAYS_MINUTE_VAL :=
V_DAYS_VAL := V_DAYS_VAL + V_DAYS_MINUTE_VAL ;
------------------------------
V_FACTOR := ( V_DAYS_MINUTE_VAL / V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE ) ;
V_DAYS_VAL := V_DAYS_VAL + V_FACTOR ;
--##-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
--## 2 GET COUNT DAYS BETWEEN SENDING DATE S FROM & TO AS OFFICIAL DAY
-- Select NVL( COUNT(*) , 0 )INTO V_OFFCIAL_Cnt From HRS_V_PRD_DAY_HLDY Where PRD_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE and P_T_DATE and BRN_NO = V_BRN_NO ;
IF FNG_GRN_PKG.IS_HLDY_DAY ( P_F_DATE => WORKING_DAYS.WRK_DATE , P_T_DATE => WORKING_DAYS.WRK_DATE , P_BRN_NO => V_BRN_NO )
SELECT H.WPRD_HOUR INTO V_OFFCAL_HOUR_VAL
AND H.WPRD_NO = WORKING_DAYS.WPRD_NO
EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_OFFCAL_HOUR_VAL := 0 ;
V_OFFCAL_MINUTE_VAL :=
p_unit_val => V_OFFCAL_HOUR_VAL ,
V_OFFCAL_VAL := V_OFFCAL_VAL + V_OFFCAL_MINUTE_VAL ;
FACTOR := ( V_OFFCAL_MINUTE_VAL / V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE ) ;
V_OFFCAL_VAL := V_OFFCAL_VAL + V_FACTOR ;
--## CLEARING PER EMPLOYEE
V_COUNT_WPRD_NO := 0;
V_ALL_WPRD_HOUR := 0;
V_FACTOR := 0;
V_OFFCAL_HOUR_VAL := 0;
V_OFFCAL_MINUTE_VAL := 0;
V_DAY_MINUTE_VAL := 0;
V_DAYS_MINUTE_VAL := 0;
V_ALL_WPRD_MINUTE := 0;
--## FINALLY PROCESSING ....
--## 1.
--## CALCULATE ACCUMULATIVE VALUES FOR WHOLE EMPLOYEE ....
V_NET_VAL := NVL(V_DAYS_VAL ,0 ) - NVL( V_OFFCAL_VAL ,0) - ( V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE /*/ V_EMP_CNT */ ) ;
--V_NET_VAL := ( V_ACCUMLTV_DAYOFF_VALUE / V_EMP_CNT) ;
--## 2.
--## CALCULATE INTERMIDEATE VALUES FOR WHOLE EMPLOYEE IN ALL BRANCHES ....
V_NET_VAL := V_NET_VAL / V_EMP_CNT ;
--## 3.
--## TRANSFER ACCUMULATIVE VALUES FOR WHOLE EMPLOYEE .... TO SPECIFIC IN TYPE FUNCTION PARAMETER
IF P_TYPE = 1 THEN -- IF VALUE IS HOUR ... SO NEED TO TRANSFER MINUTE VALUE TO HOUR VALUE
V_NET_VAL := FNG_ACTL_CLC_PKG.TRANSFER_FROM_UNIT_TO_UNIT2 ( 2,1,V_NET_VAL,1) ;
-- ELSE OR OTHERWISE THE VALUE IT IS ALREADY DAY ....
--## 4.
--## RETURN RESULT OF INTERMIDIATE EMPLOYEE WORKING DAYES
RETURN V_NET_VAL ;

Function Counter( Source_strin
in varchar2,
UnTerminated
in Boolean default FALSE,
Delimiter
in varchar2 default ','
) Return number is
iModifier
pls_integer := 0;
iOldSize
pls_integer := length(Source_string);
pls_integer;
NUMBER; -- DCN change to number
varchar2(255);
begin
if Unterminated then iModifier := 1; end if;
db1 := replace(Source_string,Delimiter);
db2 := length(db1);
db3 := (iOldSize - db2) + iModifier;
return db3;
End Counter;
function GetString (Source_string
Field_position in number,
in varchar2 default ',') return varchar2 is
iPtrEnd
pls_integer
:= 0;
iPtrStart
vcSourceStrCopy varchar2(2000)
:= Source_string;
if UnTerminated then
vcSourceStrCopy := vcSourceStrCopy||Delimiter;
end if;
if Field_Position > 1 then
iPtrStart := instr(vcSourceStrCopy,Delimiter,1,Field_Position-1)+ length(Delimiter);
else iPtrStart := 1;
end if ;
:= instr(vcSourceStrCopy,Delimiter,1,Field_Position);
return substr(vcSourceStrCopy,iPtrStart,(iPtrEnd-iPtrStart));
end GetString; /* String Version */
FUNCTION RET_VAL_AS_TABLE_RCRD ( P_VALS VARCHAR2 , P_QUERY_ID NUMBER )
v_vals_cnt Number:=0;
v_val NUMBER ;
v_vals_cnt:= Counter( P_VALS ,TRUE);
IF v_vals_cnt > 0 THEN
for j in 1..v_vals_cnt loop
v_val :=replace(GetString( P_VALS ,j,TRUE),'''','');
OUT_REC.ARTCL_NO := V_VAL;
OUT_REC.ORDR_NO := P_QUERY_ID ;
end loop;

FUNCTION CHK_WORKING_DAY(
P_Wprd_No In HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.WPRD_NO %Type ,
P_DATE In Date
) RETURN NUMBER Is
V_DAYOFF_DATE FNG_DAYOFF_DTL.DAYOFF_DATE%TYPE;
V_DAYWRK_DATE FNG_DAYOFF_DTL.DAYWRK_DATE%TYPE;
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'CHK_WORKING_DAY >> 1@ '|| P_DATE );
SELECT DAYOFF_DATE ,DAYWRK_DATE INTO V_DAYOFF_DATE ,V_DAYWRK_DATE FROM FNG_DAYOFF_MST , FNG_DAYOFF_DTL
FNG_DAYOFF_DTL.DOC_SRL = FNG_DAYOFF_MST.DOC_SRL
AND EMP_NO = P_EMP_NO
AND(DAYOFF_DATE = P_DATE OR NVL(DAYWRK_DATE , DAYOFF_DATE ) = P_DATE )
-- AND NVL(PRCSSD_FLG,0) = 0 -- CAUSE NEED TO READ SAME RECORD FOR SUBSITUTE DAY AS WORKING
AND NVL(INACTIVE,0) = 0 ;
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'CHK_WORKING_DAY >> 2@ '|| V_DAYOFF_DATE ||' '|| V_DAYWRK_DATE );
IF V_DAYOFF_DATE IS NOT NULL OR V_DAYWRK_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN
-- MARK DAY AS PROCESSED IN TABLE FNG_DAYOFF_DTL .....
UPDATE FNG_DAYOFF_DTL SET PRCSSD_FLG = 1
AND NVL(PRCSSD_FLG,0) = 0
AND DOC_SRL IN ( SELECT DOC_SRL FROM FNG_DAYOFF_MST WHERE NVL(INACTIVE,0) = 0 ) ;
IF P_DATE = V_DAYWRK_DATE THEN
--- ASSING DAYWRK DATE VALUE TO RETURN PARAMETER
V_PARENT_RLTD_DATE := V_DAYOFF_DATE ;
RETURN 2 ;
ELSIF P_DATE = V_DAYOFF_DATE THEN
-- RAISED FLG TO STOP PROCESS ALTERNATE OR WORKING PERIODS ......
------------------------------------------------------------------
V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD := 1 ;
CEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN 0 ;
V_WPRD_PRCSS_FLG Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.WPRD_PRCSS_FLG%TYPE ;
V_WORKING_DAY NUMBER ;
OR (P_Wprd_Typ = 2 AND P_Assgn_Flg = 1 ) THEN
-- ASGNMENT PERIOD
IF V_WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR = 1 THEN
V_WPRD_PRCSS_FLG := 0 ;
V_WPRD_PRCSS_FLG := 1 ;
--HERE PROCESS FOR CHECKING IF WORKING DAY IS DEFINED AS DAYOFF FOR EMPLOYEE
V_WORKING_DAY:= CHK_WORKING_DAY(
P_Wprd_No => NVL( V_WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN , P_Wprd_No ) ,
P_DATE => P_Fdate
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'Strt_Fng_Clc_Prcss*&*'|| p_WPRD_NO || ' '||P_FDATE || ' '||P_tDATE ||' V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD ='||V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD );
IF V_WORKING_DAY = 1 THEN
V_DAY_OFF_FLG := 1 ; -- TRANSLATE DAY TO DAYOFF
ELSIF V_WORKING_DAY = 2 THEN
V_DAY_OFF_FLG := 0 ; -- TRANSLATE DAYOFF TO WORKING DAY
-- IT MEAN IF VARIABLE HAVE INITIAL VALUE = 0 ... THAT CURRENT WORKING PERIOD IS PRIMARY
-- SO CAPTURE THE DAY STATUS ( WORKING OR DAYOFF )
IF V_WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR = 0 THEN
--- ASSIGN VALUE ......OF DAY STATUS FOR PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD PROCESSING .....
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
V_ALTRNT_WPRD_DAYWRK := V_DAY_OFF_FLG ;
DAY_OFF_FLG,

PROCEDURE ASSIGN_INFRACTION ( P_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP In FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP%TYPE ,
P_Brn_No In S_BRN.BRN_NO%Type
-- ASSIGN INFRACTION TYPE FOR PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD ....
---------------------------------------------------------------------
UPDATE FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST M
M.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP= P_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP
AND M.Emp_No = P_EMP_NO
AND M.F_DATE = P_F_DATE
AND M.T_DATE = P_T_DATE
AND M.WPRD_PRCSS_FLG = 1 ; -- CURRENT WORKING PERIOD PROCESSING
COMMIT ;
Procedure Strt_Fng_Clc_Prcss2
P_T_HRCHY_NO IN Fn
g_Stndr_Clc_MST.HRCHY_NO%TYPE ,
CNTR NUMBER := 0 ;
V_WPRD_NO Fng_Stndr_Clc_MST.WPRD_NO%TYPE ;
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1 HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP.ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1%TYPE ;
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO2 HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP.ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO2%TYPE ;
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO3 HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP.ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO3%TYPE ;
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO4 HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP.ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO4%TYPE ;
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5 HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP.ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5%TYPE ;
CURSOR CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc_MAIN IS
AND M.WPRD_NO BETWEEN DECODE(P_F_WPRD_NO ,NULL ,M.WPRD_NO ,P_F_WPRD_NO) AND DECODE(P_T_WPRD_NO ,NULL ,M.WPRD_NO ,P_T_WPRD_NO)
--AND ( F_DATE = P_F_Date )
AND NVL(WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR , 0) = 0 -- CHOOSE PRIMMARY WORKING PERIOD 4PPROCESSING ... ***
Order By Emp_No,WPRD_NO,F_Date ;
CURSOR CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc_PER_DAY ( P_FROM_DATE DATE ,
P_TO_DATE DATE ,
P_WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST.WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN%TYPE,
P_EMP_NO S_EMP.EMP_NO%TYPE
AND M.WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN = P_WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN
--AND ( F_DATE BETWEEN P_FROM_Date AND P_TO_Date OR T_DATE BETWEEN P_FROM_Date AND P_TO_Date )
AND ( F_DATE = P_FROM_Date AND T_DATE = P_TO_DATE )
--AND NVL(WPRD_PRCSS_FLG, 0) = 1 -- CHOOSE WORKING PERIOD 4PPROCESSING ...
Order By Emp_No,F_Date,WPRD_ASSGN_INDX ;
V_CNT NUMBER := 6 ;
V_ALTRNTV_PRCSS NUMBER := 0 ;
V_F_WPRD_NO HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.WPRD_NO%TYPE ;
V_T_WPRD_NO HRS_PRD_WRK_DFN_MST.WPRD_NO%TYPE ;
IF P_PLCY_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
IF LOAD_PLCY (P_PLCY_NO => P_PLCY_NO ) = 1 THEN
V_ALTRNTV_PRCSS := PLCY.PRCSS_ALTRNTV_WPRD ;
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'ASSIGNMENT_Prc === WRK_DAY_TYP ='|| WRK_DAY_TYP || ' P_F_WPRD_NO='||P_F_WPRD_NO || ' F_DATE='||P_F_DATE || ' T_DATE ='||P_T_DATE );
--## Assigned Primary Working Period No To Be Parents For Alternates Working Periods >>>
V_WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN := P_F_WPRD_NO ;
SELECT ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1 ,
ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1 ,
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO2 ,
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO3 ,
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO4 ,
V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5
FROM HRS_PRD_WRK_EMP D
D.EMP_NO BETWEEN NVL(P_F_Emp_No,D.EMP_NO) AND NVL(P_T_Emp_No,D.EMP_NO)
AND D.WPRD_NO BETWEEN NVL(P_F_WPRD_NO,D.WPRD_NO) AND NVL(P_T_WPRD_NO, D.WPRD_NO )
( P_F_DATE BETWEEN D.F_ACTV_DATE AND D.T_ACTV_DATE OR P_T_DATE BETWEEN D.F_ACTV_DATE AND D.T_ACTV_DATE )
( D.F_ACTV_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE OR D.T_ACTV_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE )
ELSIF WRK_DAY_TYP = 2 THEN
FROM FNG_V_SHFT_GUD D
AND D.WRK_DATE BETWEEN P_F_DATE AND P_T_DATE
AND ROWNUM = 1;
SELECT NVL(
DECODE(P_F_WPRD_NO,NULL,0,1) +
DECODE(V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1,NULL,0,1) +
DECODE(V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO2,NULL,0,1) +
DECODE(V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO3,NULL,0,1) +
DECODE(V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO4,NULL,0,1) +
DECODE(V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5,NULL,0,1)
) ,6) INTO V_CNT FROM DUAL ;
--IF V_CNT = 1 THEN V_CNT := 6 ; CNTR := 5 ; END IF; --- Mean There No Alternative Working Period Defined ....
IF V_CNT = 1 THEN
V_CNT := 1 ; --- Mean There No Alternative Working Period Defined ....
--## CHECKING IF ALTERNATIVE WORKING PERIOD ACTIVATED IN ATTENDANCE
IF V_ALTRNTV_PRCSS = 1 THEN
V_CNT := 6 ;
--## NOT GO HEAD FOR PROCESSING ALTERNATIVE WORKING PERIOD
V_CNT := 1 ;
EXIT WHEN CNTR >= V_CNT ;
IF CNTR = 0 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := P_F_WPRD_NO ;
V_WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR := 0 ; -- PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD ASSIGNED
-----------------------------------------------------------
ELSIF CNTR = 1 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO1 ;
V_WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR := 1 ; -- SECONDARY WORKING PERIOD ASSIGNED
ELSIF CNTR = 2 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO2 ;
ELSIF CNTR = 3 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO3 ;
ELSIF CNTR = 4 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO4 ;
ELSIF CNTR = 5 THEN
V_WPRD_NO := V_ALTERNATE_WPRD_NO5 ;
----------------------------7-------------------------------
V_WPRD_ASSGN_INDX := CNTR ;
IF V_WPRD_NO IS NOT NULL THEN
P_T_WPRD_NO => V_WPRD_NO , --P_T_WPRD_NO ,
P_F_WPRD_NO => V_WPRD_NO , --P_F_WPRD_NO,
P_PRD_NO => P_PRD_NO,
P_WRK_DAY_TYP => WRK_DAY_TYP
P_F_WPRD_NO => V_WPRD_NO --P_F_WPRD_NO,
P_Wprd_No =>V_WPRD_NO ,
CNTR:= V_CNT;
EXIT ; -- NO ALTERNATIVE WORLING PERIOD FOR ASSIGNMENT ORDER ....
COMMIT ; -- COMMIT STANDRD CALCULATION
CNTR:= CNTR+1;
-- CHECK IF FLAG IS RAISED FOR STOP PROCESS ALTERNATE WORKING PERIODS
-- CAUSE DAYOFF PROCESSING FOR TRANSLATE WORKING DAY RO DAY OFF
-- OR TRANSLATING DAY OFF TO WORKING DAY AS SUBSUITITUE DAY ....
IF V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD = 1 THEN
CNTR := 6 ;
V_ALTRNT_WPRD_DAYWRK := NULL;
-- RESET FLAG TO ZERO .....
V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD := 0;
V_STOP_PRCSS_ALTERNATE_WPRD := 0 ;
--RETURN ;
-- START ACTUAL PROCESS DEPENDING ON PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD ASSIGNED ....
-------------------------------------------------------------------------
FOR STNDR IN CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc_Main LOOP
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PROCESSING_SHIFT === IN MAIN , STNDR.F_DATE='|| STNDR.F_DATE || ' STNDR.T_DATE='|| STNDR.T_DATE || ' STNDR.WPRD_NO='|| STNDR.WPRD_NO);
FOR STNDR_DAY IN
CUR_Fng_Stndr_Clc_PER_DAY ( P_FROM_DATE => STNDR.F_DATE ,
P_TO_DATE => STNDR.T_DATE ,
P_WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN => STNDR.WPRD_NO ,
P_EMP_NO => STNDR.EMP_NO
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PROCESSING_SHIFT === IN DTL , STNDR_DAY.F_DATE='|| STNDR_DAY.F_DATE || ' STNDR_DAY.T_DATE='|| STNDR_DAY.T_DATE || ' STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO='|| STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO);
-- FIRST UPDATE PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD & MAKE IT READY TO PROCESS
M.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP= NULL , -- CLEARING ASSIGNED INFRACTION TYPE FOR WORKING PERIOD NO
M.WPRD_PRCSS_FLG = 1
AND M.Emp_No = STNDR.EMP_NO
AND M.WPRD_NO = STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO
AND M.F_DATE = STNDR_DAY.F_DATE
AND M.T_DATE = STNDR_DAY.T_DATE
M.PRD_NO = P_PRD_NO ;
V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP := NULL;
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'Strt_Fng_Clc_Prcss7 WPRD_NO = '|| STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO ||' '||P_F_WPRD_NO || ' '||P_F_DATE ||' '||P_t_DATE );
V_F_WPRD_NO := STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO ;
V_T_WPRD_NO := STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO ;
P_F_Emp_No => STNDR.EMP_NO ,
P_T_Emp_No => STNDR.EMP_NO ,
P_F_Date => STNDR_DAY.F_DATE,
P_T_Date => STNDR_DAY.T_DATE,
P_F_HRCHY_NO => STNDR.HRCHY_NO ,
P_T_HRCHY_NO => STNDR.HRCHY_NO ,
P_T_WPRD_NO => STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO ,
P_F_WPRD_NO => STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO ,
EXIT ; -- NEVER CONTINUE IF PROCESSING EXTRA ASSIGNMENT ....
--Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DEBUGING_ACT ( 'PROCESSING_SHIFT === IN V_CNT '|| V_CNT );
EXIT ;
--## CHECKING IF CALCULATION INFRACTION TYPE NO IS IN POLICY LIST DEFINED
--## FOR KEEP PROCESSING FOR NEXT ALTERNATIVE WORKING PERIOD
IF INSTR ( NVL(PLCY.ALTRNTV_WPRD_INFRCTN_TYP,0) , V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP ) > 0 THEN
-- SO PROCESS NEXT ALTERNATE WORKING PERIOD
-- FIRST UPDATE PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD & MAKE IT OFF FOR PROCESSING
M.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP = V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP ,
M.WPRD_PRCSS_FLG = 0
-- SECOND DELETE ACTUAL RECORDS CALCULATED FOR PROCESSING WORKING PERIOD THAT FAIL TO FIND ANY FINGERPRINT FOR EMPLOYEE
-- DELETE ACTL_DATA
Fng_Stndr_Clc_Pkg.DLT_ACTL_DATA (
P_F_Emp_No => STNDR.EMP_NO,
P_T_Emp_No => STNDR.EMP_NO,
P_F_HRCHY_NO => STNDR.HRCHY_NO,
P_T_HRCHY_NO => STNDR.HRCHY_NO,
P_T_WPRD_NO => STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO,
P_F_WPRD_NO => STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO,
-- THIRD CONTINUE FOR PROCESSING NEXT ALTERNATE WORKING PERIOD
.... FOR NEXT WPRD_NO IN LOOP
-- ...
ASSIGN_INFRACTION
P_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP => V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP ,
P_Emp_No => STNDR.EMP_NO,
P_WPRD_NO => STNDR_DAY.WPRD_NO
EXIT ; -- EXIT LOOP THROUGH RESET OF ALTERNATE PERIODS .... CAUSE WE CATCH FINGERPRINT THAT PROCESSED ...
--IF V_CNT = 1 THEN EXIT ; END IF;
-- HERE CODE FOR CHECKING IS THAT IF ABSENT IS STILL RAISED .... FOR ALL ALTERNATE WORKING PERIOD PROCESSING
-- SO APPLY PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD FOR EMPLOYEE THAT ASSIGNED TO HIM
IF V_CNT <> 1 AND V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP = 0 THEN -- MEAN NOT HAVE ANY FINGERPRINT IN ALL PRIMARY & ALTERNATE WORKING PERIODS
--## HERE CHECKING WHAT ATTENDANCE SAY WHEN FAILURE TO FIND BEST WORKING PERIOD CALCULATION FOR EMPLOYEE
--## THERE ARE TO CHOICES ...FIRST => ACCEPTED PRIMARY WORKING PERIOD TO BE CALCULATED
--## SECOND => ACCEPTED LAST ALTERNATIVE WORKING PERIOD TO BE CALCULATED
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
IF NVL(PLCY.ALTRNTV_WPRD_FAILURE_PRCSS,1 ) = 1 THEN
V_F_WPRD_NO := STNDR.WPRD_NO;
V_T_WPRD_NO := STNDR.WPRD_NO;
M.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP = NULL , -- CLEARING ASSIGNED INFRACTION TYPE FOR WORKING PERIOD NO
AND M.WPRD_NO = STNDR.WPRD_NO
AND M.F_DATE = STNDR.F_DATE
AND M.T_DATE = STNDR.T_DATE
AND M.WPRD_ASSGN_PNTR = 0 ;
P_F_Date => STNDR.F_DATE,
P_T_Date => STNDR.T_DATE,
P_T_WPRD_NO => V_F_WPRD_NO , --STNDR.WPRD_NO ,
P_F_WPRD_NO => V_T_WPRD_NO , --STNDR.WPRD_NO ,
M.WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP= V_WPRD_PRCSS_INFRCTN_TYP
IF WRK_DAY_TYP <> 3 THEN
-- HERE CODE TO DELETE STANDARD CALCULATION RECORDS OF CURRENT DAY PROCESSING
-- RECORDS THAT FAILURE WHEN PROCESSING DAY
-- CAUSE NOT FOUND ANY FINGER PRINT
DELETE FROM FNG_STNDR_CLC_MST M
AND M.WPRD_NO_PARENT_ASSGN = STNDR.WPRD_NO
AND M.WPRD_PRCSS_FLG = 0 ; -- NOT RAISE FLG
